ROBOTWIN-AI REVIEW BUNDLE


==================================================
FILE: scripts\run_temporal_multimodal_diagnostic.py
==================================================
"""
Temporal + multimodal diagnostic runtime for RoboTwin-AI.

Phase 15
--------

Temporal Fault Diagnosis

This runtime evaluates whether the existing RoboTwin-AI diagnostic
architecture can distinguish between:

    - a single diagnostic observation
    - a developing fault
    - a persistent fault
    - a stable fault
    - a healthy state

The temporal layer does NOT replace the existing DiagnosticBrain or
MultimodalDiagnosticBrain.

Architecture
------------

    RobotSimulator
          |
          v
    FaultInjector
          |
          v
    PyBulletRobotAdapter
          |
          v
       Telemetry
          |
          +----------------------+
          |                      |
          v                      v
    DiagnosticBrain       VisionObserver
          |                      |
          v                      v
 TemporalDiagnosticEngine   VisionObservation
          |                      |
          +----------+-----------+
                     |
                     v
          MultimodalDiagnosticBrain
                     |
                     v
             Final Diagnostic State

Scientific boundary
-------------------

Telemetry remains the authority for internal actuator-fault diagnosis.

Vision is treated as contextual/environmental evidence and is not allowed
to independently manufacture an internal actuator fault.

The temporal layer evaluates repeated diagnostic observations over time.
It does not invent a new fault classifier.

This module is intentionally implemented as a separate experiment runner
so the previously validated diagnostic components remain unchanged.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import logging
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from robot_twin.diagnostics.diagnostic_brain import DiagnosticBrain
from robot_twin.diagnostics.fault_injector import (
    FaultInjector,
    FaultScenario,
)
from robot_twin.diagnostics.multimodal_diagnostic_brain import (
    MultimodalDiagnosticBrain,
)
from robot_twin.diagnostics.temporal_diagnostic_engine import (
    TemporalDiagnosticEngine,
)
from robot_twin.experiments.experiment_runner import (
    ExperimentScenario,
    build_initial_scenarios,
)
from robot_twin.simulation.robot_simulator import RobotSimulator
from robot_twin.telemetry.pybullet_adapter import PyBulletRobotAdapter
from robot_twin.vision.camera import CameraConfig, RobotCamera
from robot_twin.vision.obstacle_detector import ObstacleDetector
from robot_twin.vision.vision_observer import VisionObserver


LOGGER = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# ============================================================================
# Experiment configuration
# ============================================================================

DEFAULT_JOINT_INDEX = 2

DEFAULT_HISTORY_SIZE = 10

DEFAULT_SETTLING_STEPS = 240

DEFAULT_TEMPORAL_SAMPLES = 6

DEFAULT_SAMPLE_INTERVAL_SECONDS = 0.10

DEFAULT_SCENARIO_ID = "S05"

DEFAULT_OUTPUT_DIRECTORY = Path(
    "data/experiments/temporal"
)


# ============================================================================
# Result schema
# ============================================================================


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TemporalSampleResult:
    """
    Machine-readable result for one temporal observation.
    """

    sample_index: int

    timestamp_utc: str

    ground_truth_fault: str

    joint_index: int

    fault_severity: float

    telemetry_fault: str

    telemetry_confidence: float

    multimodal_fault: str

    multimodal_confidence: float

    obstacle_detected: bool

    temporal_fault: str

    temporal_confidence: float

    temporal_state: str

    persistence_score: float

    confidence_trend: str

    developing: bool

    persistent: bool

    stable: bool


# ============================================================================
# Temporal experiment runner
# ============================================================================


class TemporalMultimodalExperiment:
    """
    Execute a controlled temporal diagnostic experiment.

    The runner intentionally reuses the existing RoboTwin-AI components
    instead of implementing replacement diagnostic logic.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        joint_index: int = DEFAULT_JOINT_INDEX,
        history_size: int = DEFAULT_HISTORY_SIZE,
        settling_steps: int = DEFAULT_SETTLING_STEPS,
        temporal_samples: int = DEFAULT_TEMPORAL_SAMPLES,
        sample_interval_seconds: float = (
            DEFAULT_SAMPLE_INTERVAL_SECONDS
        ),
        output_directory: Path = DEFAULT_OUTPUT_DIRECTORY,
    ) -> None:

        self.joint_index = self._validate_joint_index(
            joint_index
        )

        self.history_size = self._validate_history_size(
            history_size
        )

        self.settling_steps = self._validate_positive_int(
            settling_steps,
            "settling_steps",
        )

        self.temporal_samples = self._validate_positive_int(
            temporal_samples,
            "temporal_samples",
        )

        self.sample_interval_seconds = (
            self._validate_positive_float(
                sample_interval_seconds,
                "sample_interval_seconds",
            )
        )

        self.output_directory = Path(
            output_directory
        )

        self.output_directory.mkdir(
            parents=True,
            exist_ok=True,
        )

    # ========================================================================
    # Validation
    # ========================================================================

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_joint_index(
        value: int,
    ) -> int:
        """
        Validate the robot joint index.
        """

        if not isinstance(value, int):
            raise TypeError(
                "joint_index must be an integer."
            )

        if value < 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "joint_index must be non-negative."
            )

        return value

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_history_size(
        value: int,
    ) -> int:
        """
        Validate temporal history length.
        """

        if not isinstance(value, int):
            raise TypeError(
                "history_size must be an integer."
            )

        if value < 2:
            raise ValueError(
                "history_size must be at least 2."
            )

        return value

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_positive_int(
        value: int,
        name: str,
    ) -> int:
        """
        Validate a positive integer configuration value.
        """

        if not isinstance(value, int):
            raise TypeError(
                f"{name} must be an integer."
            )

        if value <= 0:
            raise ValueError(
                f"{name} must be greater than zero."
            )

        return value

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_positive_float(
        value: float,
        name: str,
    ) -> float:
        """
        Validate a positive floating-point configuration value.
        """

        try:
            value = float(value)
        except (
            TypeError,
            ValueError,
        ) as exc:
            raise TypeError(
                f"{name} must be numeric."
            ) from exc

        if value <= 0.0:
            raise ValueError(
                f"{name} must be greater than zero."
            )

        return value

    # ========================================================================
    # Scenario selection
    # ========================================================================

    @staticmethod
    def _select_scenario(
        scenarios: tuple[ExperimentScenario, ...],
        scenario_id: str,
    ) -> ExperimentScenario:
        """
        Select one experiment scenario by identifier.
        """

        for scenario in scenarios:

            if scenario.scenario_id == scenario_id:
                return scenario

        available = [
            scenario.scenario_id
            for scenario in scenarios
        ]

        raise ValueError(
            f"Scenario '{scenario_id}' was not found. "
            f"Available scenarios: {available}"
        )

    # ========================================================================
    # Fault scenario
    # ========================================================================

    @staticmethod
    def _build_fault_scenario(
        scenario: ExperimentScenario,
        injector: FaultInjector,
        joint_index: int,
        severity: float,
    ) -> FaultScenario:
        """
        Build the existing FaultInjector configuration.

        The experiment-level scenario owns the canonical fault label,
        while FaultScenario owns the actual simulation configuration.

        The FaultInjector itself remains unchanged and continues to
        consume FaultScenario through its existing apply() API.
        """

        return FaultScenario(
            fault_type=scenario.fault_type,
            joint_index=joint_index,
            severity=severity,
        )

    # ========================================================================
    # Simulation settling
    # ========================================================================

    def _settle(
        self,
        *,
        simulator: RobotSimulator,
        injector: FaultInjector,
        fault_scenario: FaultScenario,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Apply the selected fault repeatedly while physics settles.
        """

        LOGGER.info(
            "Settling simulation for %d steps.",
            self.settling_steps,
        )

        for _ in range(self.settling_steps):

            injector.apply(
                fault_scenario
            )

            simulator.step()

    # ========================================================================
    # Camera
    # ========================================================================

    @staticmethod
    def _build_camera(
        *,
        simulator: RobotSimulator,
        scenario: ExperimentScenario,
    ) -> RobotCamera:
        """
        Build the camera using the same vision-quality policy as the
        validated ScenarioExecutor.
        """

        quality = str(
            scenario.vision_quality
        ).strip().lower()

        if quality == "poor":

            config = CameraConfig(
                width=320,
                height=240,
            )

            LOGGER.info(
                "Temporal scenario %s using poor vision: 320x240.",
                scenario.scenario_id,
            )

        else:

            config = CameraConfig(
                width=640,
                height=480,
            )

            LOGGER.info(
                "Temporal scenario %s using normal vision: 640x480.",
                scenario.scenario_id,
            )

        return RobotCamera(
            simulator=simulator,
            config=config,
        )

    # ========================================================================
    # Safe conversion
    # ========================================================================

    @staticmethod
    def _safe_float(
        value: object,
    ) -> float:
        """
        Convert a value to a finite float.
        """

        try:
            result = float(value)
        except (
            TypeError,
            ValueError,
        ):
            return 0.0

        if result != result:
            return 0.0

        if result in (
            float("inf"),
            float("-inf"),
        ):
            return 0.0

        return result

    # ========================================================================
    # Sample execution
    # ========================================================================

    def run(
        self,
        scenario: ExperimentScenario,
    ) -> list[TemporalSampleResult]:
        """
        Execute the complete temporal experiment.
        """

        simulator = RobotSimulator()

        injector: FaultInjector | None = None

        adapter: PyBulletRobotAdapter | None = None

        temporal_results: list[
            TemporalSampleResult
        ] = []

        try:

            LOGGER.info(
                "Starting temporal experiment: %s",
                scenario.scenario_id,
            )

            LOGGER.info(
                "Ground truth fault: %s",
                scenario.fault_type,
            )

            # --------------------------------------------------------------
            # Shared fault state
            # --------------------------------------------------------------

            injector = FaultInjector(
                simulator
            )

            adapter = PyBulletRobotAdapter(
                simulator=simulator,
                fault_injector=injector,
            )

            # --------------------------------------------------------------
            # Connect simulator
            # --------------------------------------------------------------

            adapter.connect()

            # --------------------------------------------------------------
            # Diagnostic stack
            # --------------------------------------------------------------

            diagnostic_brain = DiagnosticBrain()

            multimodal_brain = MultimodalDiagnosticBrain(
                diagnostic_brain=diagnostic_brain
            )

            temporal_engine = TemporalDiagnosticEngine(
                diagnostic_brain=diagnostic_brain,
                history_size=self.history_size,
            )

            # --------------------------------------------------------------
            # Vision stack
            # --------------------------------------------------------------

            camera = self._build_camera(
                simulator=simulator,
                scenario=scenario,
            )

            obstacle_detector = ObstacleDetector()

            vision_observer = VisionObserver(
                camera=camera,
                obstacle_detector=obstacle_detector,
            )

            # --------------------------------------------------------------
            # Fault configuration
            # --------------------------------------------------------------

            fault_scenario = self._build_fault_scenario(
                scenario=scenario,
                injector=injector,
                joint_index=self.joint_index,
                severity=float(
                    scenario.fault_severity
                ),
            )

            injector.clear(
                joint_index=self.joint_index
            )

            # --------------------------------------------------------------
            # Physics settling
            # --------------------------------------------------------------

            self._settle(
                simulator=simulator,
                injector=injector,
                fault_scenario=fault_scenario,
            )

            # --------------------------------------------------------------
            # Target position
            # --------------------------------------------------------------

            target_position = injector.get_target_position(
                joint_index=self.joint_index
            )

            LOGGER.info(
                "Target position: %.6f",
                target_position,
            )

            # --------------------------------------------------------------
            # Temporal observation loop
            # --------------------------------------------------------------

            for sample_index in range(
                1,
                self.temporal_samples + 1,
            ):

                loop_start = time.perf_counter()

                # ----------------------------------------------------------
                # Keep the fault active
                # ----------------------------------------------------------

                injector.apply(
                    fault_scenario
                )

                simulator.step()

                # ----------------------------------------------------------
                # Live telemetry
                # ----------------------------------------------------------

                telemetry = adapter.get_joint_telemetry(
                    joint_index=self.joint_index
                )

                # ----------------------------------------------------------
                # Temporal diagnosis
                # ----------------------------------------------------------

                temporal_result = temporal_engine.update(
                    telemetry=telemetry,
                    target_position=target_position,
                )

                # ----------------------------------------------------------
                # Independent telemetry diagnosis
                #
                # This is intentionally kept visible so we can compare
                # single-snapshot diagnosis against temporal diagnosis.
                # ----------------------------------------------------------

                telemetry_result = diagnostic_brain.diagnose(
                    telemetry=telemetry,
                    target_position=target_position,
                )

                # ----------------------------------------------------------
                # Vision observation
                # ----------------------------------------------------------

                vision_observation = (
                    vision_observer.observe()
                )

                # ----------------------------------------------------------
                # Multimodal diagnosis
                # ----------------------------------------------------------

                multimodal_result = multimodal_brain.diagnose(
                    diagnostic_result=telemetry_result,
                    vision_observation=vision_observation,
                )

                # ----------------------------------------------------------
                # Result extraction
                # ----------------------------------------------------------

                result = TemporalSampleResult(
                    sample_index=sample_index,

                    timestamp_utc=(
                        datetime.now(
                            timezone.utc
                        ).isoformat()
                    ),

                    ground_truth_fault=str(
                        scenario.fault_type.value
                        if hasattr(
                            scenario.fault_type,
                            "value",
                        )
                        else scenario.fault_type
                    ),

                    joint_index=self.joint_index,

                    fault_severity=self._safe_float(
                        scenario.fault_severity
                    ),

                    telemetry_fault=str(
                        telemetry_result.fault_type
                    ),

                    telemetry_confidence=self._safe_float(
                        telemetry_result.confidence
                    ),

                    multimodal_fault=str(
                        multimodal_result.fault_type
                    ),

                    multimodal_confidence=self._safe_float(
                        multimodal_result.combined_score
                    ),

                    obstacle_detected=bool(
                        vision_observation.obstacle_detected
                    ),

                    temporal_fault=str(
                        temporal_result.fault_type
                    ),

                    temporal_confidence=self._safe_float(
                        temporal_result.confidence
                    ),

                    temporal_state=str(
                        temporal_result.state
                    ),

                    persistence_score=self._safe_float(
                        temporal_result.persistence_score
                    ),

                    confidence_trend=str(
                        temporal_result.trend
                    ),

                    developing=bool(
                        temporal_result.developing
                    ),

                    persistent=bool(
                        temporal_result.persistent
                    ),

                    stable=bool(
                        temporal_result.stable
                    ),
                )

                temporal_results.append(
                    result
                )

                # ----------------------------------------------------------
                # Research-readable console output
                # ----------------------------------------------------------

                print()

                print(
                    f"[TEMPORAL SAMPLE {sample_index:02d}/"
                    f"{self.temporal_samples:02d}]"
                )

                print(
                    "-" * 72
                )

                print(
                    f"Ground truth       : "
                    f"{result.ground_truth_fault}"
                )

                print(
                    f"Telemetry fault    : "
                    f"{result.telemetry_fault}"
                )

                print(
                    f"Telemetry conf.    : "
                    f"{result.telemetry_confidence:.4f}"
                )

                print(
                    f"Multimodal fault   : "
                    f"{result.multimodal_fault}"
                )

                print(
                    f"Multimodal conf.   : "
                    f"{result.multimodal_confidence:.4f}"
                )

                print(
                    f"Temporal fault     : "
                    f"{result.temporal_fault}"
                )

                print(
                    f"Temporal conf.     : "
                    f"{result.temporal_confidence:.4f}"
                )

                print(
                    f"Temporal state     : "
                    f"{result.temporal_state}"
                )

                print(
                    f"Persistence        : "
                    f"{result.persistence_score:.4f}"
                )

                print(
                    f"Confidence trend   : "
                    f"{result.confidence_trend}"
                )

                print(
                    f"Developing         : "
                    f"{result.developing}"
                )

                print(
                    f"Persistent         : "
                    f"{result.persistent}"
                )

                print(
                    f"Stable             : "
                    f"{result.stable}"
                )

                print(
                    f"Obstacle           : "
                    f"{result.obstacle_detected}"
                )

                print(
                    "-" * 72
                )

                # ----------------------------------------------------------
                # Sampling interval
                # ----------------------------------------------------------

                elapsed = (
                    time.perf_counter()
                    - loop_start
                )

                remaining = (
                    self.sample_interval_seconds
                    - elapsed
                )

                if remaining > 0.0:

                    time.sleep(
                        remaining
                    )

            return temporal_results

        finally:

            # --------------------------------------------------------------
            # Fault cleanup
            # --------------------------------------------------------------

            if injector is not None:

                try:

                    injector.clear(
                        joint_index=self.joint_index
                    )

                except Exception:

                    LOGGER.exception(
                        "Fault cleanup failed."
                    )

            # --------------------------------------------------------------
            # Simulator cleanup
            # --------------------------------------------------------------

            if adapter is not None:

                try:

                    adapter.disconnect()

                except Exception:

                    LOGGER.exception(
                        "Simulator cleanup failed."
                    )

    # ========================================================================
    # Final summary
    # ========================================================================

    @staticmethod
    def print_final_summary(
        results: list[TemporalSampleResult],
    ) -> None:
        """
        Print the final temporal diagnosis summary.
        """

        if not results:

            raise ValueError(
                "No temporal results were produced."
            )

        final = results[-1]

        print()
        print()

        print(
            "ROBOTWIN-AI TEMPORAL MULTIMODAL DIAGNOSIS"
        )

        print(
            "=" * 72
        )

        print(
            f"Ground Truth       : "
            f"{final.ground_truth_fault}"
        )

        print(
            f"Final Telemetry    : "
            f"{final.telemetry_fault}"
        )

        print(
            f"Final Multimodal   : "
            f"{final.multimodal_fault}"
        )

        print(
            f"Final Temporal     : "
            f"{final.temporal_fault}"
        )

        print(
            f"Final State        : "
            f"{final.temporal_state}"
        )

        print(
            f"Final Confidence   : "
            f"{final.temporal_confidence:.4f}"
        )

        print(
            f"Persistence Score  : "
            f"{final.persistence_score:.4f}"
        )

        print(
            f"Confidence Trend   : "
            f"{final.confidence_trend}"
        )

        print(
            f"Developing         : "
            f"{final.developing}"
        )

        print(
            f"Persistent         : "
            f"{final.persistent}"
        )

        print(
            f"Stable             : "
            f"{final.stable}"
        )

        print(
            "=" * 72
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Scientific interpretation
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        print()

        if final.persistent:

            print(
                "TEMPORAL CONCLUSION : PERSISTENT FAULT"
            )

            print(
                "Repeated observations consistently "
                "support the diagnosed fault."
            )

        elif final.developing:

            print(
                "TEMPORAL CONCLUSION : DEVELOPING FAULT"
            )

            print(
                "The fault is beginning to persist across "
                "multiple observations."
            )

        elif final.stable:

            print(
                "TEMPORAL CONCLUSION : STABLE FAULT"
            )

            print(
                "The diagnosed fault and confidence remain "
                "stable over time."
            )

        else:

            print(
                "TEMPORAL CONCLUSION : SINGLE / INSUFFICIENT"
            )

            print(
                "More temporal observations are required "
                "for a stronger persistence decision."
            )

        print()

        print(
            "Observation count  : "
            f"{len(results)}"
        )

        print(
            "=" * 72
        )

    # ========================================================================
    # JSON persistence
    # ========================================================================

    def save_results(
        self,
        results: list[TemporalSampleResult],
        scenario: ExperimentScenario,
    ) -> Path:
        """
        Persist temporal experiment results as JSON.
        """

        import json
        from dataclasses import asdict

        if not results:

            raise ValueError(
                "results must contain at least one item."
            )

        output_path = (
            self.output_directory
            /
            (
                f"temporal_"
                f"{scenario.scenario_id.lower()}.json"
            )
        )

        payload = {

            "experiment": (
                "RoboTwin-AI temporal multimodal "
                "diagnostic experiment"
            ),

            "phase": (
                "Phase 15 - Temporal Fault Diagnosis"
            ),

            "created_at_utc": (
                datetime.now(
                    timezone.utc
                ).isoformat()
            ),

            "configuration": {

                "joint_index": self.joint_index,

                "history_size": self.history_size,

                "settling_steps": self.settling_steps,

                "temporal_samples": self.temporal_samples,

                "sample_interval_seconds": (
                    self.sample_interval_seconds
                ),
            },

            "scenario": {

                "scenario_id": scenario.scenario_id,

                "scenario_name": scenario.name,

                "fault_type": str(
                    scenario.fault_type.value
                    if hasattr(
                        scenario.fault_type,
                        "value",
                    )
                    else scenario.fault_type
                ),

                "fault_severity": (
                    scenario.fault_severity
                ),

                "obstacle_present": bool(
                    scenario.obstacle_present
                ),

                "vision_quality": str(
                    scenario.vision_quality
                ),
            },

            "results": [
                asdict(result)
                for result in results
            ],

            "scientific_boundary": {

                "internal_fault_authority": (
                    "telemetry"
                ),

                "vision_role": (
                    "environmental/contextual evidence"
                ),

                "temporal_role": (
                    "repeated-observation persistence "
                    "and confidence trend analysis"
                ),
            },
        }

        output_path.write_text(
            json.dumps(
                payload,
                indent=2,
                sort_keys=True,
            ),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )

        LOGGER.info(
            "Temporal results saved to %s",
            output_path,
        )

        return output_path


# ============================================================================
# Main
# ============================================================================


def main() -> None:
    """
    Execute one controlled temporal diagnostic experiment.
    """

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Command-line scenario selection
    #
    # Default remains S05, so running the script without arguments preserves
    # the previous behavior.
    #
    # Example:
    #
    #   python scripts/run_temporal_multimodal_diagnostic.py --scenario S06
    #
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=(
            "Run one RoboTwin-AI temporal "
            "multimodal diagnostic scenario."
        )
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--scenario",
        default=DEFAULT_SCENARIO_ID,
        help=(
            "Scenario ID to run, for example "
            "S01, S02, S03, S04, S05 or S06."
        ),
    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    logging.basicConfig(
        level=logging.INFO,
        format=(
            "%(asctime)s | "
            "%(levelname)s | "
            "%(message)s"
        ),
    )

    print()

    print(
        "ROBOTWIN-AI PHASE 15"
    )

    print(
        "TEMPORAL MULTIMODAL DIAGNOSTIC EXPERIMENT"
    )

    print(
        "=" * 72
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Load existing controlled scenarios
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    scenarios = build_initial_scenarios()

    scenario = TemporalMultimodalExperiment._select_scenario(
        scenarios=scenarios,
        scenario_id=args.scenario,
    )

    print(
        f"Scenario           : "
        f"{scenario.scenario_id}"
    )

    print(
        f"Fault              : "
        f"{scenario.fault_type}"
    )

    print(
        f"Severity            : "
        f"{scenario.fault_severity}"
    )

    print(
        f"Vision quality      : "
        f"{scenario.vision_quality}"
    )

    print(
        f"Temporal samples    : "
        f"{DEFAULT_TEMPORAL_SAMPLES}"
    )

    print(
        f"History size        : "
        f"{DEFAULT_HISTORY_SIZE}"
    )

    print(
        "=" * 72
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Create experiment
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    experiment = TemporalMultimodalExperiment(
        joint_index=DEFAULT_JOINT_INDEX,
        history_size=DEFAULT_HISTORY_SIZE,
        settling_steps=DEFAULT_SETTLING_STEPS,
        temporal_samples=DEFAULT_TEMPORAL_SAMPLES,
        sample_interval_seconds=(
            DEFAULT_SAMPLE_INTERVAL_SECONDS
        ),
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Run experiment
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    results = experiment.run(
        scenario
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final diagnosis
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    experiment.print_final_summary(
        results
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Save machine-readable results
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    output_path = experiment.save_results(
        results=results,
        scenario=scenario,
    )

    print()

    print(
        f"Results saved to: {output_path}"
    )

    print(
        "=" * 72
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


==================================================
FILE: src\robot_twin\experiments\experiment_runner.py
==================================================

"""
Controlled scientific experiment runner for RoboTwin-AI.

This module provides the first research-grade experiment layer for comparing:

    1. Telemetry-only diagnosis
    2. Vision-only diagnosis
    3. Multimodal diagnosis

The runner deliberately separates:

    - experimental condition
    - ground-truth fault
    - observed telemetry
    - observed vision
    - system prediction
    - confidence
    - execution latency

This separation is required for reproducible evaluation and prevents
the evaluation layer from deriving ground truth from model predictions.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
from enum import Enum
import csv
import math
from pathlib import Path
import time
from typing import Iterable

from robot_twin.diagnostics.fault_injector import FaultType


class DiagnosticMode(str, Enum):
    """Diagnostic systems being compared."""

    TELEMETRY_ONLY = "telemetry_only"
    VISION_ONLY = "vision_only"
    MULTIMODAL = "multimodal"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ExperimentScenario:
    """
    Defines one controlled experimental condition.

    Ground truth belongs to the scenario definition, not to the
    diagnostic model.
    """

    scenario_id: str
    name: str
    fault_type: FaultType
    obstacle_present: bool = False
    vision_quality: str = "normal"
    seed: int = 0
    fault_severity: float = 0.70

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.scenario_id.strip():
            raise ValueError("scenario_id cannot be empty.")

        if not self.name.strip():
            raise ValueError("name cannot be empty.")

        if self.seed < 0:
            raise ValueError("seed cannot be negative.")

        if self.vision_quality not in {
            "normal",
            "poor",
            "unavailable",
        }:
            raise ValueError(
                "vision_quality must be one of: "
                "normal, poor, unavailable."
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ExperimentObservation:
    """
    Raw evidence collected for one experimental run.

    This object intentionally contains observations rather than predictions.
    """

    joint_index: int

    telemetry_available: bool
    vision_available: bool

    telemetry_anomaly_score: float
    vision_anomaly_score: float
    vision_reliability: float

    obstacle_detected: bool

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.joint_index < 0:
            raise ValueError("joint_index cannot be negative.")

        for name, value in (
            ("telemetry_anomaly_score", self.telemetry_anomaly_score),
            ("vision_anomaly_score", self.vision_anomaly_score),
            ("vision_reliability", self.vision_reliability),
        ):
            if not math.isfinite(float(value)):
                raise ValueError(f"{name} must be finite.")

            if not 0.0 <= float(value) <= 1.0:
                raise ValueError(f"{name} must be between 0 and 1.")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ExperimentPrediction:
    """Prediction produced by one diagnostic mode."""

    scenario_id: str
    mode: DiagnosticMode

    ground_truth: FaultType
    predicted_fault: FaultType

    confidence: float
    anomaly: bool

    latency_seconds: float

    obstacle_detected: bool
    vision_reliability: float

    correct: bool

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not 0.0 <= self.confidence <= 1.0:
            raise ValueError("confidence must be between 0 and 1.")

        if self.latency_seconds < 0.0:
            raise ValueError("latency_seconds cannot be negative.")


class ExperimentRunner:
    """
    Executes and records controlled RoboTwin-AI experiments.

    This class intentionally does not contain robot-specific fault logic.

    The simulator, telemetry system, vision system, and diagnostic brains
    remain responsible for producing observations and predictions.

    The experiment runner is responsible for scientific bookkeeping.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        output_directory: str | Path = "data/experiments",
    ) -> None:
        self.output_directory = Path(output_directory)
        self.output_directory.mkdir(
            parents=True,
            exist_ok=True,
        )

        self._results: list[ExperimentPrediction] = []

    @property
    def results(self) -> tuple[ExperimentPrediction, ...]:
        """Return immutable experiment results."""

        return tuple(self._results)

    def record_prediction(
        self,
        *,
        scenario: ExperimentScenario,
        mode: DiagnosticMode,
        predicted_fault: FaultType,
        confidence: float,
        anomaly: bool,
        latency_seconds: float,
        observation: ExperimentObservation,
    ) -> ExperimentPrediction:
        """
        Record one model prediction.

        Ground truth is copied directly from the controlled scenario.
        """

        prediction = ExperimentPrediction(
            scenario_id=scenario.scenario_id,
            mode=mode,
            ground_truth=scenario.fault_type,
            predicted_fault=predicted_fault,
            confidence=self._validate_confidence(confidence),
            anomaly=bool(anomaly),
            latency_seconds=float(latency_seconds),
            obstacle_detected=observation.obstacle_detected,
            vision_reliability=observation.vision_reliability,
            correct=predicted_fault == scenario.fault_type,
        )

        self._results.append(prediction)

        return prediction

    def save_results(
        self,
        filename: str = "experiment_results.csv",
    ) -> Path:
        """Persist all recorded predictions as a CSV dataset."""

        if not filename.endswith(".csv"):
            raise ValueError("filename must end with .csv")

        output_path = self.output_directory / filename

        fieldnames = [
            "scenario_id",
            "mode",
            "ground_truth",
            "predicted_fault",
            "confidence",
            "anomaly",
            "latency_seconds",
            "obstacle_detected",
            "vision_reliability",
            "correct",
        ]

        with output_path.open(
            "w",
            newline="",
            encoding="utf-8",
        ) as file:
            writer = csv.DictWriter(
                file,
                fieldnames=fieldnames,
            )

            writer.writeheader()

            for result in self._results:
                row = asdict(result)

                row["mode"] = result.mode.value
                row["ground_truth"] = result.ground_truth.value
                row["predicted_fault"] = result.predicted_fault.value

                writer.writerow(row)

        return output_path

    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Clear recorded experiment results."""

        self._results.clear()

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_confidence(value: float) -> float:
        value = float(value)

        if not math.isfinite(value):
            raise ValueError("confidence must be finite.")

        if not 0.0 <= value <= 1.0:
            raise ValueError("confidence must be between 0 and 1.")

        return value


def build_initial_scenarios() -> tuple[ExperimentScenario, ...]:
    """
    Build the first controlled experiment matrix.

    These scenarios are deliberately small and reproducible.
    Additional repetitions and operating conditions will be added
    after the baseline pipeline is validated.
    """

    return (
        ExperimentScenario(
            scenario_id="S01",
            name="Healthy baseline",
            fault_type=FaultType.NORMAL,
            seed=1001,
        ),
        ExperimentScenario(
            scenario_id="S02",
            name="Motor resistance",
            fault_type=FaultType.MOTOR_RESISTANCE,
            seed=1002,
        ),
        ExperimentScenario(
            scenario_id="S03",
            name="Low motor power",
            fault_type=FaultType.LOW_MOTOR_POWER,
            seed=1003,
        ),
        ExperimentScenario(
            scenario_id="S04",
            name="Position drift",
            fault_type=FaultType.POSITION_DRIFT,
            seed=1004,
        ),
        ExperimentScenario(
            scenario_id="S05",
            name="Motor resistance with obstacle",
            fault_type=FaultType.MOTOR_RESISTANCE,
            obstacle_present=True,
            seed=1005,
        ),
        ExperimentScenario(
            scenario_id="S06",
            name="Motor resistance with poor vision",
            fault_type=FaultType.MOTOR_RESISTANCE,
            vision_quality="poor",
            seed=1006,
        ),
    )


def main() -> None:
    """Run a bookkeeping-only validation of the experiment framework."""

    runner = ExperimentRunner()

    scenarios = build_initial_scenarios()

    print("ROBOTWIN-AI EXPERIMENT FRAMEWORK")
    print("=" * 60)

    for scenario in scenarios:
        print(
            f"{scenario.scenario_id} | "
            f"{scenario.name} | "
            f"fault={scenario.fault_type.value} | "
            f"vision={scenario.vision_quality} | "
            f"obstacle={scenario.obstacle_present} | "
            f"seed={scenario.seed}"
        )

    print()
    print(f"Scenarios defined: {len(scenarios)}")
    print(f"Output directory : {runner.output_directory}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()




==================================================
FILE: src\robot_twin\diagnostics\baseline_anomaly_detector.py
==================================================

"""Baseline-based anomaly detection for RoboTwin-AI."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Final

from robot_twin.telemetry.robot_adapter import JointTelemetry


DEFAULT_BASELINE_FILE: Final[Path] = (
    Path("data") / "healthy_baseline.json"
)

DEFAULT_Z_SCORE_THRESHOLD: Final[float] = 3.0
MIN_STANDARD_DEVIATION: Final[float] = 1e-9

POSITION_ERROR_WARNING_THRESHOLD: Final[float] = 0.20
POSITION_ERROR_SEVERE_THRESHOLD: Final[float] = 1.00


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FeatureBaseline:
    """Statistical baseline for one telemetry feature."""

    mean: float
    standard_deviation: float


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BaselineAnomalyResult:
    """Result produced by baseline anomaly detection."""

    is_anomaly: bool
    anomaly_score: float
    reasons: list[str]
    z_scores: dict[str, float]


class BaselineAnomalyDetector:
    """Detect telemetry anomalies using a healthy robot baseline."""

    _ANALYZED_FEATURES: Final[tuple[str, ...]] = (
        "velocity",
        "force",
        "motor_current",
        "temperature",
        "position_error",
    )

    # Position error is command-dependent, so it is not loaded
    # from the fixed per-joint baseline.
    _BASELINE_FEATURES: Final[tuple[str, ...]] = (
        "velocity",
        "force",
        "motor_current",
        "temperature",
    )

    def __init__(
        self,
        baseline_file: Path = DEFAULT_BASELINE_FILE,
        z_score_threshold: float = DEFAULT_Z_SCORE_THRESHOLD,
    ) -> None:
        """Initialize the detector and load the baseline."""

        self._baseline_file = Path(baseline_file)
        self._z_score_threshold = float(z_score_threshold)

        self._validate_configuration()
        self._baseline = self._load_baseline()

    def detect(
        self,
        telemetry: JointTelemetry,
        target_position: float,
    ) -> BaselineAnomalyResult:
        """Compare telemetry with the baseline."""

        joint_baseline = self._get_joint_baseline(
            int(telemetry.joint_index)
        )

        target_position = float(target_position)

        # Keep the same error sign convention used by the
        # project's diagnostic brain.
        position_error = (
            float(telemetry.position) - target_position
        )

        actual_values = {
            "velocity": float(telemetry.velocity),
            "force": float(telemetry.force),
            "motor_current": float(telemetry.motor_current),
            "temperature": float(telemetry.temperature),
            "position_error": position_error,
        }

        z_scores: dict[str, float] = {}
        reasons: list[str] = []
        physical_z_scores: list[float] = []

        for feature_name in self._ANALYZED_FEATURES:

            actual_value = actual_values[feature_name]

            # Position error does not have fixed baseline statistics.
            # It is checked separately below.
            if feature_name == "position_error":
                z_scores[feature_name] = 0.0
                continue

            statistics = joint_baseline[feature_name]

            z_score = self._calculate_z_score(
                actual_value=actual_value,
                baseline=statistics,
            )

            z_scores[feature_name] = z_score
            physical_z_scores.append(abs(z_score))

            if abs(z_score) >= self._z_score_threshold:
                reasons.append(
                    self._build_reason(
                        feature_name=feature_name,
                        z_score=z_score,
                        actual_value=actual_value,
                        baseline=statistics,
                    )
                )

        # Evaluate command tracking independently from the
        # physical-feature baseline.
        absolute_position_error = abs(position_error)
        position_error_score = 0.0

        if (
            absolute_position_error
            >= POSITION_ERROR_SEVERE_THRESHOLD
        ):
            position_error_score = 1.0

            reasons.append(
                "Command tracking error is severe "
                f"(actual={telemetry.position:.4f}, "
                f"target={target_position:.4f}, "
                f"position_error={position_error:.4f})."
            )

        elif (
            absolute_position_error
            >= POSITION_ERROR_WARNING_THRESHOLD
        ):
            position_error_score = 0.50

            reasons.append(
                "Command tracking error exceeds the warning "
                f"threshold (actual={telemetry.position:.4f}, "
                f"target={target_position:.4f}, "
                f"position_error={position_error:.4f})."
            )

        maximum_physical_z = max(
            physical_z_scores,
            default=0.0,
        )

        physical_score = min(
            maximum_physical_z
            / (self._z_score_threshold * 2.0),
            1.0,
        )

        anomaly_score = max(
            physical_score,
            position_error_score,
        )

        return BaselineAnomalyResult(
            is_anomaly=bool(reasons),
            anomaly_score=float(anomaly_score),
            reasons=reasons,
            z_scores=z_scores,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _calculate_z_score(
        actual_value: float,
        baseline: FeatureBaseline,
    ) -> float:
        """Calculate a numerically safe z-score."""

        standard_deviation = max(
            abs(float(baseline.standard_deviation)),
            MIN_STANDARD_DEVIATION,
        )

        return float(
            (
                float(actual_value)
                - float(baseline.mean)
            )
            / standard_deviation
        )

    def _get_joint_baseline(
        self,
        joint_index: int,
    ) -> dict[str, FeatureBaseline]:
        """Return baseline statistics for one joint."""

        if joint_index not in self._baseline:
            raise ValueError(
                f"No baseline found for joint {joint_index} "
                f"in '{self._baseline_file}'."
            )

        return self._baseline[joint_index]

    def _load_baseline(
        self,
    ) -> dict[int, dict[str, FeatureBaseline]]:
        """Load baseline statistics from JSON.

        Supports both standard_deviation and legacy std keys.
        """

        if not self._baseline_file.exists():
            raise FileNotFoundError(
                "Baseline file not found: "
                f"{self._baseline_file}"
            )

        try:
            with self._baseline_file.open(
                "r",
                encoding="utf-8",
            ) as file:
                payload = json.load(file)

        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise ValueError(
                "Baseline file contains invalid JSON: "
                f"{self._baseline_file}"
            ) from exc

        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
            raise ValueError(
                "Baseline JSON root must be an object."
            )

        joints = payload.get("joints")

        if not isinstance(joints, list):
            raise ValueError(
                "Baseline file does not contain a valid "
                "'joints' list."
            )

        baseline: dict[
            int,
            dict[str, FeatureBaseline],
        ] = {}

        for joint in joints:

            if not isinstance(joint, dict):
                raise ValueError(
                    "Each item in 'joints' must be an object."
                )

            if "joint_index" not in joint:
                raise ValueError(
                    "A joint entry is missing 'joint_index'."
                )

            joint_index = int(joint["joint_index"])

            if joint_index in baseline:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Duplicate baseline entry for joint "
                    f"{joint_index}."
                )

            feature_baselines: dict[
                str,
                FeatureBaseline,
            ] = {}

            for feature_name in self._BASELINE_FEATURES:

                statistics = joint.get(feature_name)

                if not isinstance(statistics, dict):
                    raise ValueError(
                        "Missing baseline statistics for "
                        f"joint {joint_index}, "
                        f"feature '{feature_name}'."
                    )

                if "mean" not in statistics:
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"Missing 'mean' for joint "
                        f"{joint_index}, feature "
                        f"'{feature_name}'."
                    )

                # Accept both supported JSON formats.
                if "standard_deviation" in statistics:
                    raw_standard_deviation = (
                        statistics["standard_deviation"]
                    )

                elif "std" in statistics:
                    raw_standard_deviation = (
                        statistics["std"]
                    )

                else:
                    raise ValueError(
                        "Missing 'standard_deviation' or "
                        f"'std' for joint {joint_index}, "
                        f"feature '{feature_name}'."
                    )

                mean = float(statistics["mean"])

                standard_deviation = float(
                    raw_standard_deviation
                )

                if not math.isfinite(mean):
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"Non-finite mean for joint "
                        f"{joint_index}, feature "
                        f"'{feature_name}'."
                    )

                if not math.isfinite(standard_deviation):
                    raise ValueError(
                        "Non-finite standard deviation for "
                        f"joint {joint_index}, "
                        f"feature '{feature_name}'."
                    )

                # Constant or near-constant signals are allowed.
                # Protect the denominator instead of rejecting
                # the entire baseline.
                standard_deviation = max(
                    abs(standard_deviation),
                    MIN_STANDARD_DEVIATION,
                )

                feature_baselines[feature_name] = (
                    FeatureBaseline(
                        mean=mean,
                        standard_deviation=standard_deviation,
                    )
                )

            baseline[joint_index] = feature_baselines

        if not baseline:
            raise ValueError(
                "Baseline file does not contain "
                "any joint statistics."
            )

        return baseline

    @staticmethod
    def _build_reason(
        feature_name: str,
        z_score: float,
        actual_value: float,
        baseline: FeatureBaseline,
    ) -> str:
        """Create a human-readable anomaly explanation."""

        direction = (
            "above"
            if z_score > 0
            else "below"
        )

        return (
            f"{feature_name} is {direction} the "
            f"healthy baseline "
            f"(actual={actual_value:.4f}, "
            f"baseline_mean={baseline.mean:.4f}, "
            f"z_score={z_score:.2f})."
        )

    def _validate_configuration(self) -> None:
        """Validate detector configuration."""

        if not math.isfinite(self._z_score_threshold):
            raise ValueError(
                "z_score_threshold must be finite."
            )

        if self._z_score_threshold <= 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "z_score_threshold must be greater "
                "than zero."
            )


def main() -> None:
    """Load the baseline and run command-aware smoke tests."""

    detector = BaselineAnomalyDetector()

    print()
    print("ROBOTWIN-AI BASELINE ANOMALY DETECTOR")
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"Baseline file : {detector._baseline_file}")
    print(f"Joints loaded : {len(detector._baseline)}")
    print("Baseline loaded successfully.")

    healthy_telemetry = JointTelemetry(
        joint_index=2,
        position=0.8,
        velocity=0.0,
        force=0.34,
        motor_current=0.34,
        temperature=27.7,
    )

    abnormal_telemetry = JointTelemetry(
        joint_index=2,
        position=0.5,
        velocity=0.1,
        force=25.0,
        motor_current=2.8,
        temperature=61.0,
    )

    healthy_result = detector.detect(
        telemetry=healthy_telemetry,
        target_position=0.8,
    )

    abnormal_result = detector.detect(
        telemetry=abnormal_telemetry,
        target_position=0.8,
    )

    print()
    print("HEALTHY COMMAND-AWARE TEST")
    print("-" * 70)
    print(
        f"Anomaly detected : "
        f"{healthy_result.is_anomaly}"
    )
    print(
        f"Anomaly score    : "
        f"{healthy_result.anomaly_score:.4f}"
    )
    print(f"Z-scores         : {healthy_result.z_scores}")

    print()
    print("ABNORMAL COMMAND-AWARE TEST")
    print("-" * 70)
    print(
        f"Anomaly detected : "
        f"{abnormal_result.is_anomaly}"
    )
    print(
        f"Anomaly score    : "
        f"{abnormal_result.anomaly_score:.4f}"
    )
    print(f"Z-scores         : {abnormal_result.z_scores}")

    for reason in abnormal_result.reasons:
        print(f"- {reason}")

    print("=" * 70)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()




==================================================
FILE: src\robot_twin\diagnostics\temporal_diagnostic_engine.py
==================================================
"""Temporal diagnostic reasoning for RoboTwin-AI.

This module adds time-aware reasoning on top of the existing
DiagnosticBrain without modifying the existing diagnostic pipeline.

Flow:

    telemetry snapshot
            â†“
      DiagnosticBrain
            â†“
       diagnosis result
            â†“
    TemporalDiagnosticEngine
            â†“
    history + persistence + trend
            â†“
      temporal diagnosis
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections import deque
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Deque, Optional


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TemporalDiagnosis:
    """Time-aware diagnostic result."""

    fault_type: str
    confidence: float

    history_size: int
    persistence_score: float

    state: str
    trend: str

    developing: bool
    persistent: bool
    stable: bool

    message: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TemporalObservation:
    """One diagnostic observation stored in temporal history."""

    fault_type: str
    confidence: float
    position: float
    velocity: float
    force: float
    motor_current: float
    temperature: float


class TemporalDiagnosticEngine:
    """Add temporal reasoning around the existing DiagnosticBrain.

    The existing DiagnosticBrain remains responsible for diagnosing
    individual telemetry snapshots.

    This class only reasons about repeated observations over time.
    """

    DEFAULT_HISTORY_SIZE = 10

    MIN_PERSISTENCE_COUNT = 3
    DEVELOPING_COUNT = 2

    CONFIDENCE_INCREASE_THRESHOLD = 0.03
    CONFIDENCE_DECREASE_THRESHOLD = 0.03

    def __init__(
        self,
        diagnostic_brain: Any,
        history_size: int = DEFAULT_HISTORY_SIZE,
    ) -> None:
        if history_size < 2:
            raise ValueError(
                "history_size must be at least 2."
            )

        self._diagnostic_brain = diagnostic_brain

        self._history: Deque[
            TemporalObservation
        ] = deque(
            maxlen=history_size
        )

    @property
    def history(self) -> tuple[TemporalObservation, ...]:
        """Return immutable temporal history."""

        return tuple(self._history)

    def reset(self) -> None:
        """Clear temporal history."""

        self._history.clear()

    def update(
        self,
        telemetry: Any,
        target_position: float,
        vision_observation: Optional[Any] = None,
    ) -> TemporalDiagnosis:
        """Diagnose one snapshot and update temporal reasoning."""

        diagnosis = self._diagnose_snapshot(
            telemetry=telemetry,
            target_position=target_position,
            vision_observation=vision_observation,
        )

        observation = TemporalObservation(
            fault_type=self._extract_fault_type(
                diagnosis
            ),
            confidence=self._extract_confidence(
                diagnosis
            ),
            position=self._extract_float(
                telemetry,
                "position",
            ),
            velocity=self._extract_float(
                telemetry,
                "velocity",
            ),
            force=self._extract_float(
                telemetry,
                "force",
            ),
            motor_current=self._extract_float(
                telemetry,
                "motor_current",
            ),
            temperature=self._extract_float(
                telemetry,
                "temperature",
            ),
        )

        self._history.append(observation)

        return self._build_temporal_diagnosis()

    def _diagnose_snapshot(
        self,
        telemetry: Any,
        target_position: float,
        vision_observation: Optional[Any],
    ) -> Any:
        """Use the existing DiagnosticBrain API."""

        if vision_observation is None:
            return self._diagnostic_brain.diagnose(
                telemetry,
                target_position,
            )

        return self._diagnostic_brain.diagnose(
            telemetry,
            target_position,
            vision_observation=vision_observation,
        )

    def _build_temporal_diagnosis(
        self,
    ) -> TemporalDiagnosis:
        """Build a temporal diagnosis from history."""

        if not self._history:
            return TemporalDiagnosis(
                fault_type="UNKNOWN",
                confidence=0.0,
                history_size=0,
                persistence_score=0.0,
                state="NO_DATA",
                trend="UNKNOWN",
                developing=False,
                persistent=False,
                stable=False,
                message="No telemetry history available.",
            )

        latest = self._history[-1]

        persistence_score = (
            self._calculate_persistence_score()
        )

        trend = self._calculate_confidence_trend()

        persistent = (
            len(self._history)
            >= self.MIN_PERSISTENCE_COUNT
            and persistence_score >= 0.67
        )

        developing = (
            len(self._history)
            >= self.DEVELOPING_COUNT
            and latest.fault_type != "NORMAL"
            and persistence_score >= 0.50
        )

        stable = (
            latest.fault_type != "NORMAL"
            and persistent
            and trend == "STABLE"
        )

        state = self._determine_state(
            latest_fault=latest.fault_type,
            developing=developing,
            persistent=persistent,
            stable=stable,
        )

        message = self._build_message(
            fault_type=latest.fault_type,
            confidence=latest.confidence,
            state=state,
            trend=trend,
            persistence_score=persistence_score,
        )

        return TemporalDiagnosis(
            fault_type=latest.fault_type,
            confidence=latest.confidence,
            history_size=len(self._history),
            persistence_score=persistence_score,
            state=state,
            trend=trend,
            developing=developing,
            persistent=persistent,
            stable=stable,
            message=message,
        )

    def _calculate_persistence_score(self) -> float:
        """Calculate how consistently the latest fault appears."""

        if not self._history:
            return 0.0

        latest_fault = self._history[-1].fault_type

        matching = sum(
            observation.fault_type == latest_fault
            for observation in self._history
        )

        return matching / len(self._history)

    def _calculate_confidence_trend(self) -> str:
        """Determine whether confidence is rising, falling, or stable."""

        if len(self._history) < 2:
            return "UNKNOWN"

        previous = self._history[-2].confidence
        current = self._history[-1].confidence

        delta = current - previous

        if delta >= self.CONFIDENCE_INCREASE_THRESHOLD:
            return "INCREASING"

        if delta <= -self.CONFIDENCE_DECREASE_THRESHOLD:
            return "DECREASING"

        return "STABLE"

    @staticmethod
    def _determine_state(
        latest_fault: str,
        developing: bool,
        persistent: bool,
        stable: bool,
    ) -> str:
        """Determine temporal state."""

        if latest_fault == "NORMAL":
            return "HEALTHY"

        if stable:
            return "STABLE_FAULT"

        if persistent:
            return "PERSISTENT_FAULT"

        if developing:
            return "DEVELOPING_FAULT"

        return "SINGLE_OBSERVATION"

    @staticmethod
    def _build_message(
        fault_type: str,
        confidence: float,
        state: str,
        trend: str,
        persistence_score: float,
    ) -> str:
        """Create a human-readable temporal diagnosis."""

        if state == "HEALTHY":
            return (
                "Robot telemetry is currently healthy."
            )

        if state == "DEVELOPING_FAULT":
            return (
                f"{fault_type} appears to be developing. "
                f"Confidence={confidence:.3f}, "
                f"persistence={persistence_score:.2f}, "
                f"trend={trend}."
            )

        if state == "PERSISTENT_FAULT":
            return (
                f"{fault_type} is persistent across recent "
                f"telemetry observations. "
                f"Confidence={confidence:.3f}, "
                f"persistence={persistence_score:.2f}."
            )

        if state == "STABLE_FAULT":
            return (
                f"{fault_type} remains stable across the "
                f"recent telemetry window. "
                f"Confidence={confidence:.3f}."
            )

        return (
            f"{fault_type} detected from the latest "
            f"observation. Temporal evidence is still limited."
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _extract_fault_type(
        diagnosis: Any,
    ) -> str:
        """Extract fault type from an existing diagnosis object."""

        candidates = (
            "fault_type",
            "ml_fault_type",
            "predicted_fault",
            "diagnosis",
        )

        for attribute in candidates:
            value = getattr(
                diagnosis,
                attribute,
                None,
            )

            if value is None:
                continue

            if hasattr(value, "value"):
                value = value.value

            return str(value)

        if isinstance(diagnosis, dict):
            for key in candidates:
                if key in diagnosis:
                    value = diagnosis[key]

                    if hasattr(value, "value"):
                        value = value.value

                    return str(value)

        return "UNKNOWN"

    @staticmethod
    def _extract_confidence(
        diagnosis: Any,
    ) -> float:
        """Extract confidence from an existing diagnosis object."""

        candidates = (
            "confidence",
            "ml_confidence",
            "diagnostic_confidence",
        )

        for attribute in candidates:
            value = getattr(
                diagnosis,
                attribute,
                None,
            )

            if value is not None:
                return float(value)

        if isinstance(diagnosis, dict):
            for key in candidates:
                if key in diagnosis:
                    return float(
                        diagnosis[key]
                    )

        return 0.0

    @staticmethod
    def _extract_float(
        telemetry: Any,
        attribute: str,
    ) -> float:
        """Safely extract a numeric telemetry value."""

        value = getattr(
            telemetry,
            attribute,
            0.0,
        )

        if value is None:
            return 0.0

        return float(value)


==================================================
FILE: src\robot_twin\diagnostics\diagnostic_brain.py
==================================================

"""
Multimodal AI diagnostic orchestration for RoboTwin-AI.

The diagnostic brain combines:

    - telemetry anomaly detection
    - machine-learning fault classification
    - deterministic telemetry fault signatures
    - deterministic fault evidence
    - camera / vision observations

Architecture
------------

Telemetry is the primary diagnostic modality for internal actuator
faults.

Vision provides:

    - environmental context
    - obstacle information
    - observation-quality information

Vision must not independently declare an internal actuator fault.

The telemetry API remains backward compatible:

    brain.diagnose(
        telemetry,
        target_position,
    )

Optional vision:

    brain.diagnose(
        telemetry,
        target_position,
        vision_observation,
    )

Diagnostic pipeline:

    telemetry
        |
        +--> baseline anomaly detection
        +--> deterministic telemetry signature
        +--> ML fault classification
        +--> deterministic fault evidence
        +--> optional vision context
        +--> final diagnosis
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import math
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Final

from robot_twin.diagnostics.baseline_anomaly_detector import (
    BaselineAnomalyDetector,
)

from robot_twin.diagnostics.fault_classifier import (
    FaultClassifier,
)

from robot_twin.diagnostics.fault_evidence_engine import (
    FaultEvidence,
    FaultEvidenceEngine,
)

from robot_twin.telemetry.robot_adapter import JointTelemetry

from robot_twin.vision.vision_observer import (
    VisionObservation,
)


LOGGER = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Diagnostic thresholds
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

DEFAULT_HIGH_SEVERITY_SCORE: Final[float] = 0.80
DEFAULT_MEDIUM_SEVERITY_SCORE: Final[float] = 0.50
DEFAULT_CLASSIFIER_CONFIDENCE: Final[float] = 0.60
DEFAULT_EVIDENCE_SCORE: Final[float] = 0.50
DEFAULT_DEGRADED_EVIDENCE_SCORE: Final[float] = 0.50

DEFAULT_MAX_VISION_CONFIDENCE_BONUS: Final[float] = 0.08
DEFAULT_MAX_VISION_CONFIDENCE_PENALTY: Final[float] = 0.05


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Telemetry signature thresholds
#
# These thresholds are diagnostic thresholds for the controlled
# RoboTwin-AI simulation.
#
# They are NOT physical robot specifications.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

POSITION_ERROR_SIGNATURE_LIMIT: Final[float] = 0.05

LOW_POWER_CURRENT_LIMIT: Final[float] = 3.00
LOW_POWER_CURRENT_MIN: Final[float] = 0.50
LOW_POWER_FORCE_LIMIT: Final[float] = 10.0

RESISTANCE_CURRENT_LIMIT: Final[float] = 4.50
RESISTANCE_FORCE_LIMIT: Final[float] = 20.0

TEMPERATURE_HIGH_LIMIT: Final[float] = 65.0


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Controlled POSITION_DRIFT signature
#
# S04 in the current digital-twin scenario produces:
#
#     position_error ~= -0.05
#     force           ~= 0.90
#     current         ~= 0.53
#     temperature     ~= 29 C
#
# S01 NORMAL has almost the same position/force values, but its
# motor current remains below 0.50.
#
# S03 LOW_MOTOR_POWER has higher current and higher temperature.
#
# Therefore this narrow signature separates S04 without changing
# the existing S01/S02/S03 rules.
#
# These are simulation-control thresholds, NOT physical specifications.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

POSITION_DRIFT_REVERSE_ERROR_LIMIT: Final[float] = 0.05
POSITION_DRIFT_LOW_CURRENT_LIMIT: Final[float] = 0.75
POSITION_DRIFT_MIN_CURRENT: Final[float] = 0.50
POSITION_DRIFT_LOW_FORCE_LIMIT: Final[float] = 10.0
POSITION_DRIFT_COOL_TEMPERATURE_LIMIT: Final[float] = 32.0
POSITION_DRIFT_FLOAT_TOLERANCE = 1e-9


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Fault categories
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

UNKNOWN_FAULTS: Final[frozenset[str]] = frozenset(
    {
        "UNKNOWN",
        "NORMAL",
    }
)

SUPPORTED_FAULTS: Final[frozenset[str]] = frozenset(
    {
        "NORMAL",
        "MOTOR_RESISTANCE",
        "LOW_MOTOR_POWER",
        "POSITION_DRIFT",
        "UNKNOWN",
    }
)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Result
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DiagnosticResult:
    """Structured diagnostic result for one robot joint."""

    joint_index: int

    status: str

    fault_type: str

    severity: str

    confidence: float

    anomaly_score: float

    ml_fault_type: str

    ml_confidence: float

    evidence: tuple[str, ...]

    probable_cause: str

    recommended_action: str

    vision_available: bool = False

    vision_status: str = "UNAVAILABLE"

    vision_reliability: float = 0.0

    vision_evidence: tuple[str, ...] = ()

    fusion_mode: str = "TELEMETRY_ONLY"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Diagnostic Brain
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class DiagnosticBrain:
    """
    Explainable telemetry + vision diagnostic engine.

    Decision authority:

        1. Strong ML prediction + telemetry agreement
        2. Strong telemetry signature
        3. Healthy consensus between ML and evidence
        4. Strong deterministic evidence
        5. ML prediction when telemetry is inconclusive
        6. Degraded deterministic evidence
        7. Conservative reason-based fallback
        8. UNKNOWN

    Vision never independently creates an actuator-fault diagnosis.

    POSITION ERROR CONVENTION
    -------------------------

        position_error = actual_position - commanded_position

        position_error < 0
            actual position is below commanded position

        position_error > 0
            actual position is above commanded position
    """

    _CAUSES: Final[dict[str, str]] = {
        "NORMAL": (
            "No significant abnormal robot behavior was detected."
        ),

        "MOTOR_RESISTANCE": (
            "Possible mechanical resistance, increased external "
            "load, or motor degradation affecting the joint."
        ),

        "LOW_MOTOR_POWER": (
            "Possible insufficient motor power, actuator weakness, "
            "or power delivery problem."
        ),

        "POSITION_DRIFT": (
            "Possible position-control error, sensor drift, "
            "calibration problem, or joint tracking issue."
        ),

        "UNKNOWN": (
            "The available telemetry and environmental observations "
            "do not provide enough evidence to determine a specific "
            "root cause."
        ),
    }

    _RECOMMENDATIONS: Final[dict[str, str]] = {
        "NORMAL": (
            "Continue normal operation and monitoring."
        ),

        "MOTOR_RESISTANCE": (
            "Inspect the affected joint for mechanical resistance, "
            "unexpected load, obstruction, or motor degradation."
        ),

        "LOW_MOTOR_POWER": (
            "Inspect motor power delivery, actuator performance, "
            "electrical connections, and controller output."
        ),

        "POSITION_DRIFT": (
            "Inspect position feedback, calibration, encoder "
            "health, and joint control."
        ),

        "UNKNOWN": (
            "Collect additional telemetry and camera observations "
            "before taking corrective action."
        ),
    }

    def __init__(
        self,
        anomaly_detector: BaselineAnomalyDetector | None = None,
        fault_classifier: FaultClassifier | None = None,
        evidence_engine: FaultEvidenceEngine | None = None,
        high_severity_score: float = DEFAULT_HIGH_SEVERITY_SCORE,
        medium_severity_score: float = DEFAULT_MEDIUM_SEVERITY_SCORE,
        classifier_confidence_threshold: float = (
            DEFAULT_CLASSIFIER_CONFIDENCE
        ),
        evidence_score_threshold: float = DEFAULT_EVIDENCE_SCORE,
        degraded_evidence_score_threshold: float = (
            DEFAULT_DEGRADED_EVIDENCE_SCORE
        ),
        max_vision_confidence_bonus: float = (
            DEFAULT_MAX_VISION_CONFIDENCE_BONUS
        ),
        max_vision_confidence_penalty: float = (
            DEFAULT_MAX_VISION_CONFIDENCE_PENALTY
        ),
    ) -> None:

        if not (
            0.0
            < medium_severity_score
            < high_severity_score
            <= 1.0
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Severity thresholds must satisfy "
                "0 < medium < high <= 1."
            )

        if not (
            0.0
            < classifier_confidence_threshold
            <= 1.0
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Classifier confidence threshold must "
                "satisfy 0 < threshold <= 1."
            )

        if not (
            0.0
            < evidence_score_threshold
            <= 1.0
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Evidence score threshold must "
                "satisfy 0 < threshold <= 1."
            )

        if not (
            0.0
            < degraded_evidence_score_threshold
            <= 1.0
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Degraded evidence score threshold must "
                "satisfy 0 < threshold <= 1."
            )

        if not (
            0.0
            <= max_vision_confidence_bonus
            <= 0.25
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Maximum vision confidence bonus must "
                "satisfy 0 <= bonus <= 0.25."
            )

        if not (
            0.0
            <= max_vision_confidence_penalty
            <= 0.25
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Maximum vision confidence penalty must "
                "satisfy 0 <= penalty <= 0.25."
            )

        self._anomaly_detector = (
            anomaly_detector
            if anomaly_detector is not None
