            else BaselineAnomalyDetector()
        )

        self._fault_classifier = (
            fault_classifier
            if fault_classifier is not None
            else FaultClassifier()
        )

        self._evidence_engine = (
            evidence_engine
            if evidence_engine is not None
            else FaultEvidenceEngine()
        )

        self._high_severity_score = float(
            high_severity_score
        )

        self._medium_severity_score = float(
            medium_severity_score
        )

        self._classifier_confidence_threshold = float(
            classifier_confidence_threshold
        )

        self._evidence_score_threshold = float(
            evidence_score_threshold
        )

        self._degraded_evidence_score_threshold = float(
            degraded_evidence_score_threshold
        )

        self._max_vision_confidence_bonus = float(
            max_vision_confidence_bonus
        )

        self._max_vision_confidence_penalty = float(
            max_vision_confidence_penalty
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Public API
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def diagnose(
        self,
        telemetry: JointTelemetry,
        target_position: float,
        vision_observation: VisionObservation | None = None,
    ) -> DiagnosticResult:

        if not isinstance(
            telemetry,
            JointTelemetry,
        ):
            raise TypeError(
                "telemetry must be a JointTelemetry instance."
            )

        target_position = self._validate_numeric(
            target_position,
            "target_position",
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Baseline anomaly detection
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        anomaly_result = (
            self._anomaly_detector.detect(
                telemetry=telemetry,
                target_position=target_position,
            )
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Deterministic telemetry signature
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        telemetry_signature = (
            self._infer_fault_signature(
                telemetry=telemetry,
                target_position=target_position,
            )
        )

        normalized_signature = (
            self._normalize_fault_type(
                telemetry_signature
            )
        )

        signature_valid = (
            normalized_signature
            not in UNKNOWN_FAULTS
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Vision context
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        vision_context = self._analyze_vision(
            vision_observation
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Healthy telemetry
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        if (
            not anomaly_result.is_anomaly
            and not signature_valid
        ):
            return self._build_normal_result(
                telemetry=telemetry,
                anomaly_score=(
                    anomaly_result.anomaly_score
                ),
                vision_context=vision_context,
            )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # ML prediction
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        ml_fault_type, ml_confidence = (
            self._predict_fault(
                telemetry=telemetry,
                target_position=target_position,
            )
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Deterministic evidence
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        evidence_result = (
            self._analyze_fault_evidence(
                telemetry=telemetry,
                target_position=target_position,
            )
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Final fault selection
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        fault_type = self._select_fault_type(
            ml_fault_type=ml_fault_type,
            ml_confidence=ml_confidence,
            evidence_result=evidence_result,
            telemetry_signature=telemetry_signature,
            reasons=anomaly_result.reasons,
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Vision context
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        fault_type = self._apply_vision_context(
            fault_type=fault_type,
            evidence_result=evidence_result,
            vision_context=vision_context,
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Explainability
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        evidence = self._build_evidence(
            reasons=anomaly_result.reasons,
            ml_fault_type=ml_fault_type,
            ml_confidence=ml_confidence,
            evidence_result=evidence_result,
            telemetry_signature=telemetry_signature,
            vision_context=vision_context,
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Severity
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        if fault_type == "NORMAL":
            severity = "LOW"
        else:
            severity = self._calculate_severity(
                anomaly_score=anomaly_result.anomaly_score,
            )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Confidence
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        confidence = self._calculate_confidence(
            anomaly_score=anomaly_result.anomaly_score,
            ml_confidence=ml_confidence,
            evidence_score=evidence_result.score,
            evidence_count=len(evidence),
            telemetry_signature=telemetry_signature,
            final_fault_type=fault_type,
        )

        confidence = self._fuse_vision_confidence(
            base_confidence=confidence,
            fault_type=fault_type,
            vision_context=vision_context,
            evidence_result=evidence_result,
        )

        fusion_mode = self._determine_fusion_mode(
            vision_context
        )

        final_status = (
            "NORMAL"
            if fault_type == "NORMAL"
            else "ANOMALY"
        )

        return DiagnosticResult(
            joint_index=telemetry.joint_index,
            status=final_status,
            fault_type=fault_type,
            severity=severity,
            confidence=round(
                confidence,
                4,
            ),
            anomaly_score=round(
                anomaly_result.anomaly_score,
                4,
            ),
            ml_fault_type=ml_fault_type,
            ml_confidence=round(
                ml_confidence,
                4,
            ),
            evidence=evidence,
            probable_cause=self._CAUSES.get(
                fault_type,
                self._CAUSES["UNKNOWN"],
            ),
            recommended_action=self._RECOMMENDATIONS.get(
                fault_type,
                self._RECOMMENDATIONS["UNKNOWN"],
            ),
            vision_available=bool(
                vision_context["available"]
            ),
            vision_status=str(
                vision_context["status"]
            ),
            vision_reliability=round(
                float(
                    vision_context["reliability"]
                ),
                4,
            ),
            vision_evidence=(
                vision_context["evidence"]
                if isinstance(
                    vision_context["evidence"],
                    tuple,
                )
                else ()
            ),
            fusion_mode=fusion_mode,
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Normal result
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _build_normal_result(
        self,
        telemetry: JointTelemetry,
        anomaly_score: float,
        vision_context: dict[str, object],
    ) -> DiagnosticResult:

        evidence: list[str] = [
            "Telemetry remains within the learned healthy baseline."
        ]

        vision_evidence = vision_context["evidence"]

        if isinstance(
            vision_evidence,
            tuple,
        ):
            evidence.extend(
                vision_evidence
            )

        return DiagnosticResult(
            joint_index=telemetry.joint_index,
            status="NORMAL",
            fault_type="NORMAL",
            severity="LOW",
            confidence=round(
                max(
                    0.0,
                    min(
                        1.0 - anomaly_score,
                        1.0,
                    ),
                ),
                4,
            ),
            anomaly_score=round(
                anomaly_score,
                4,
            ),
            ml_fault_type="NORMAL",
            ml_confidence=1.0,
            evidence=tuple(evidence),
            probable_cause=self._CAUSES["NORMAL"],
            recommended_action=(
                self._RECOMMENDATIONS["NORMAL"]
            ),
            vision_available=bool(
                vision_context["available"]
            ),
            vision_status=str(
                vision_context["status"]
            ),
            vision_reliability=round(
                float(
                    vision_context["reliability"]
                ),
                4,
            ),
            vision_evidence=(
                vision_evidence
                if isinstance(
                    vision_evidence,
                    tuple,
                )
                else ()
            ),
            fusion_mode=self._determine_fusion_mode(
                vision_context
            ),
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # ML classifier
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _predict_fault(
        self,
        telemetry: JointTelemetry,
        target_position: float,
    ) -> tuple[str, float]:

        try:
            result = (
                self._fault_classifier
                .predict_with_confidence(
                    telemetry=telemetry,
                    commanded_position=target_position,
                )
            )

            predicted_fault, confidence = (
                self._parse_classifier_result(
                    result
                )
            )

        except Exception as exc:
            LOGGER.warning(
                "Fault classifier prediction failed: %s",
                exc,
            )

            return (
                "UNKNOWN",
                0.0,
            )

        normalized_fault = (
            self._normalize_fault_type(
                predicted_fault
            )
        )

        try:
            confidence = float(
                confidence
            )
        except (
            TypeError,
            ValueError,
        ):
            confidence = 0.0

        confidence = max(
            0.0,
            min(
                confidence,
                1.0,
            ),
        )

        return (
            normalized_fault,
            confidence,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _parse_classifier_result(
        result: object,
    ) -> tuple[object, object]:

        if isinstance(
            result,
            tuple,
        ) and len(result) >= 2:
            return (
                result[0],
                result[1],
            )

        if isinstance(
            result,
            list,
        ) and len(result) >= 2:
            return (
                result[0],
                result[1],
            )

        if isinstance(
            result,
            dict,
        ):
            fault = (
                result.get("fault_type")
                or result.get("prediction")
                or result.get("label")
                or "UNKNOWN"
            )

            confidence = (
                result.get("confidence")
                or result.get("score")
                or 0.0
            )

            return (
                fault,
                confidence,
            )

        fault = (
            getattr(
                result,
                "fault_type",
                None,
            )
            or getattr(
                result,
                "prediction",
                None,
            )
            or "UNKNOWN"
        )

        confidence = getattr(
            result,
            "confidence",
            0.0,
        )

        return (
            fault,
            confidence,
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Deterministic evidence
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _analyze_fault_evidence(
        self,
        telemetry: JointTelemetry,
        target_position: float,
    ) -> FaultEvidence:

        position_error = (
            telemetry.position
            - target_position
        )

        return (
            self._evidence_engine
            .get_best_evidence(
                position_error=position_error,
                velocity=abs(
                    telemetry.velocity
                ),
                force=abs(
                    telemetry.force
                ),
                motor_current=max(
                    0.0,
                    telemetry.motor_current,
                ),
                temperature=telemetry.temperature,
            )
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Telemetry signature
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _infer_fault_signature(
        self,
        telemetry: JointTelemetry,
        target_position: float,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Infer a controlled fault category directly from telemetry.

        The S04 position-drift case is intentionally handled as a
        narrow controlled-digital-twin signature.

        This prevents changing the already validated S01/S02/S03
        diagnostic rules.
        """

        position_error = (
            telemetry.position
            - target_position
        )

        force = abs(
            telemetry.force
        )

        current = max(
            0.0,
            telemetry.motor_current,
        )

        temperature = max(
            0.0,
            telemetry.temperature,
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # MOTOR RESISTANCE
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        if (
            force >= RESISTANCE_FORCE_LIMIT
            and current >= RESISTANCE_CURRENT_LIMIT
        ):
            return "MOTOR_RESISTANCE"

        if (
            temperature >= TEMPERATURE_HIGH_LIMIT
            and current >= RESISTANCE_CURRENT_LIMIT
        ):
            return "MOTOR_RESISTANCE"

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # POSITION DRIFT
        #
        # Existing positive-error rule remains unchanged.
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        if (
            position_error
            >= POSITION_ERROR_SIGNATURE_LIMIT
            and current
            <= LOW_POWER_CURRENT_LIMIT
        ):
            return "POSITION_DRIFT"

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # CONTROLLED S04 POSITION DRIFT
        #
        # S04 signature:
        #
        #   small negative tracking error
        #   low actuator force
        #   current just above healthy idle current
        #   cool motor
        #
        # S01:
        #   current < 0.50
        #
        # S03:
        #   current > 0.75 and temperature > 32 C
        #
        # Therefore this rule does not overlap with the validated
        # S01/S03 signatures.
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        if (
            position_error
            <= -(POSITION_DRIFT_REVERSE_ERROR_LIMIT
                 - POSITION_DRIFT_FLOAT_TOLERANCE
            )
                 
            and force
            <= POSITION_DRIFT_LOW_FORCE_LIMIT
            and POSITION_DRIFT_MIN_CURRENT
            < current
            <= POSITION_DRIFT_LOW_CURRENT_LIMIT
            and temperature
            < POSITION_DRIFT_COOL_TEMPERATURE_LIMIT
        ):
            return "POSITION_DRIFT"

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # LOW MOTOR POWER
        #
        # Existing rule remains unchanged.
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        if (
            position_error
            <= -POSITION_ERROR_SIGNATURE_LIMIT
            and force
            <= LOW_POWER_FORCE_LIMIT
            and LOW_POWER_CURRENT_MIN
            < current
            <= LOW_POWER_CURRENT_LIMIT
        ):
            return "LOW_MOTOR_POWER"

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # UNKNOWN
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        return "UNKNOWN"

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Fault selection
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _select_fault_type(
        self,
        ml_fault_type: str,
        ml_confidence: float,
        evidence_result: FaultEvidence,
        telemetry_signature: str,
        reasons: tuple[str, ...],
    ) -> str:

        normalized_ml_fault = (
            self._normalize_fault_type(
                ml_fault_type
            )
        )

        normalized_signature = (
            self._normalize_fault_type(
                telemetry_signature
            )
        )

        normalized_evidence = (
            self._normalize_fault_type(
                evidence_result.fault_type
            )
        )

        ml_valid = (
            normalized_ml_fault
            not in UNKNOWN_FAULTS
        )

        signature_valid = (
            normalized_signature
            not in UNKNOWN_FAULTS
        )

        evidence_fault_valid = (
            normalized_evidence
            not in UNKNOWN_FAULTS
        )

        ml_confident = (
            ml_valid
            and ml_confidence
            >= self._classifier_confidence_threshold
        )

        evidence_strong = (
            evidence_fault_valid
            and evidence_result.score
            >= self._evidence_score_threshold
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # 1. Strong ML + telemetry signature agreement
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        if (
            ml_confident
            and signature_valid
            and normalized_ml_fault
            == normalized_signature
        ):
            LOGGER.debug(
                "ML and telemetry signature agree: %s",
                normalized_ml_fault,
            )

            return normalized_ml_fault

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # 2. Strong deterministic telemetry signature
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        if signature_valid:

            if (
                ml_confident
                and normalized_ml_fault
                != normalized_signature
            ):
                LOGGER.warning(
                    "ML/signature disagreement | ML=%s "
                    "confidence=%.4f | telemetry=%s. "
                    "Using telemetry-consistent diagnosis.",
                    normalized_ml_fault,
                    ml_confidence,
                    normalized_signature,
                )

            return normalized_signature

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # 3. Healthy consensus
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        if (
            normalized_ml_fault == "NORMAL"
            and normalized_evidence == "NORMAL"
        ):
            return "NORMAL"

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # 4. Strong deterministic evidence
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        if evidence_strong:
            return normalized_evidence

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # 5. Confident ML prediction
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        if ml_confident:
            return normalized_ml_fault

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # 6. Degraded deterministic evidence
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        if (
            evidence_fault_valid
            and evidence_result.score
            >= self._degraded_evidence_score_threshold
        ):
            return normalized_evidence

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # 7. Conservative fallback
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        return self._infer_fault_type_from_reasons(
            reasons=reasons
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _infer_fault_type_from_reasons(
        reasons: tuple[str, ...],
    ) -> str:

        reason_text = " ".join(
            reasons
        ).lower()

        if (
            "force"
            in reason_text
            and "current"
            in reason_text
        ):
            return "MOTOR_RESISTANCE"

        if "current" in reason_text:
            return "LOW_MOTOR_POWER"

        if "position" in reason_text:
            return "POSITION_DRIFT"

        return "UNKNOWN"

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Vision
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _analyze_vision(
        self,
        vision_observation: VisionObservation | None,
    ) -> dict[str, object]:

        if vision_observation is None:
            return {
                "available": False,
                "status": "UNAVAILABLE",
                "reliability": 0.0,
                "obstacle_detected": False,
                "obstacle_distance": float("inf"),
                "evidence": (),
            }

        reliability = (
            self._calculate_vision_reliability(
                vision_observation
            )
        )

        evidence: list[str] = []

        if not vision_observation.visibility_ok:
            evidence.append(
                "Vision quality is degraded; visual observations "
                "should not be treated as strong diagnostic evidence."
            )

        if not vision_observation.depth_available:
            evidence.append(
                "Depth data is unavailable."
            )

        if (
            vision_observation.depth_available
            and vision_observation.valid_depth_ratio < 0.50
        ):
            evidence.append(
                "Depth coverage is low "
                f"({vision_observation.valid_depth_ratio:.4f})."
            )

        if vision_observation.segmentation_available:
            evidence.append(
                "Vision segmentation is available with "
                f"{vision_observation.visible_object_count} "
                "visible object IDs."
            )

        if vision_observation.obstacle_detected:

            distance = (
                vision_observation.obstacle_distance
            )

            if self._is_finite_non_negative(
                distance
            ):
                evidence.append(
                    "Visual obstacle detected at approximately "
                    f"{distance:.3f} m."
                )
            else:
                evidence.append(
                    "Visual obstacle detected, but its distance "
                    "measurement is unavailable."
                )

        else:
            evidence.append(
                "No obstacle was detected inside the configured "
                "visual danger region."
            )

        if reliability >= 0.80:
            status = "HEALTHY"
        elif reliability >= 0.50:
            status = "DEGRADED"
        else:
            status = "LOW_CONFIDENCE"

        return {
            "available": True,
            "status": status,
            "reliability": reliability,
            "obstacle_detected": (
                vision_observation.obstacle_detected
            ),
            "obstacle_distance": (
                vision_observation.obstacle_distance
            ),
            "evidence": tuple(evidence),
        }

    @staticmethod
    def _calculate_vision_reliability(
        observation: VisionObservation,
    ) -> float:

        visibility_score = (
            1.0
            if observation.visibility_ok
            else 0.0
        )

        depth_score = 0.0

        if observation.depth_available:
            depth_score = max(
                0.0,
                min(
                    float(
                        observation.valid_depth_ratio
                    ),
                    1.0,
                ),
            )

        segmentation_score = (
            1.0
            if observation.segmentation_available
            else 0.0
        )

        reliability = (
            0.30 * visibility_score
            + 0.50 * depth_score
            + 0.20 * segmentation_score
        )

        return round(
            max(
                0.0,
                min(
                    reliability,
                    1.0,
                ),
            ),
            4,
        )

    def _apply_vision_context(
        self,
        fault_type: str,
        evidence_result: FaultEvidence,
        vision_context: dict[str, object],
    ) -> str:

        normalized_fault = (
            self._normalize_fault_type(
                fault_type
            )
        )

        evidence_fault = (
            self._normalize_fault_type(
                evidence_result.fault_type
            )
        )

        obstacle_detected = bool(
            vision_context[
                "obstacle_detected"
            ]
        )

        reliability = float(
            vision_context[
                "reliability"
            ]
        )

        if (
            obstacle_detected
            and reliability >= 0.50
            and (
                normalized_fault
                == "MOTOR_RESISTANCE"
                or evidence_fault
                == "MOTOR_RESISTANCE"
            )
        ):
            if normalized_fault == "UNKNOWN":
                return "MOTOR_RESISTANCE"

        return normalized_fault

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Explainability
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _build_evidence(
        self,
        reasons: tuple[str, ...],
        ml_fault_type: str,
        ml_confidence: float,
        evidence_result: FaultEvidence,
        telemetry_signature: str,
        vision_context: dict[str, object],
    ) -> tuple[str, ...]:

        evidence: list[str] = []

        if reasons:
            evidence.extend(
                (
                    "Baseline deviation detected: "
                    f"{reason}."
                )
                for reason in reasons
            )
        else:
            evidence.append(
                "Telemetry deviates from the learned "
                "healthy baseline or matched a deterministic "
                "fault signature."
            )

        evidence.append(
            "ML classifier prediction: "
            f"{ml_fault_type} "
            f"(confidence={ml_confidence:.4f})."
        )

        if (
            ml_confidence
            < self._classifier_confidence_threshold
        ):
            evidence.append(
                "ML classifier confidence is below "
                "the diagnostic acceptance threshold."
            )

        normalized_signature = (
            self._normalize_fault_type(
                telemetry_signature
            )
        )

        evidence.append(
            "Telemetry signature inference: "
            f"{normalized_signature}."
        )

        normalized_evidence_fault = (
            self._normalize_fault_type(
                evidence_result.fault_type
            )
        )

        evidence.append(
            "Fault evidence engine prediction: "
            f"{normalized_evidence_fault} "
            f"(score={evidence_result.score:.4f})."
        )

        for reason in evidence_result.reasons:
            evidence.append(
                f"Evidence engine: {reason}"
            )

        vision_evidence = (
            vision_context["evidence"]
        )

        if isinstance(
            vision_evidence,
            tuple,
        ):
            for reason in vision_evidence:
                evidence.append(
                    f"Vision evidence: {reason}"
                )

        reliability = float(
            vision_context[
                "reliability"
            ]
        )

        if reliability > 0.0:
            evidence.append(
                "Vision reliability: "
                f"{reliability:.4f}."
            )

        return tuple(evidence)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Confidence
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @staticmethod
    def _calculate_confidence(
        anomaly_score: float,
        ml_confidence: float,
        evidence_score: float,
        evidence_count: int,
        telemetry_signature: str,
        final_fault_type: str,
    ) -> float:

        evidence_factor = min(
            evidence_count / 6.0,
            1.0,
        )

        normalized_signature = str(
            telemetry_signature
        ).upper()

        normalized_final = str(
            final_fault_type
        ).upper()

        signature_agreement = (
            1.0
            if (
                normalized_signature
                not in UNKNOWN_FAULTS
                and normalized_signature
                == normalized_final
            )
            else 0.0
        )

        confidence = (
            0.30 * anomaly_score
            + 0.25 * ml_confidence
            + 0.25 * evidence_score
            + 0.10 * evidence_factor
            + 0.10 * signature_agreement
        )

        return round(
            max(
                0.0,
                min(
                    confidence,
                    1.0,
                ),
            ),
            4,
        )

    def _fuse_vision_confidence(
        self,
        base_confidence: float,
        fault_type: str,
        vision_context: dict[str, object],
        evidence_result: FaultEvidence,
    ) -> float:

        confidence = max(
            0.0,
            min(
                base_confidence,
                1.0,
            ),
        )

        if not bool(
            vision_context["available"]
        ):
            return round(
                confidence,
                4,
            )

        reliability = float(
            vision_context[
                "reliability"
            ]
        )

        if reliability <= 0.0:
            return round(
                confidence,
                4,
            )

        obstacle_detected = bool(
            vision_context[
                "obstacle_detected"
            ]
        )

        evidence_fault = (
            self._normalize_fault_type(
                evidence_result.fault_type
            )
        )

        normalized_fault = (
            self._normalize_fault_type(
                fault_type
            )
        )

        vision_supports_resistance = (
            obstacle_detected
            and reliability >= 0.50
            and (
                normalized_fault
                == "MOTOR_RESISTANCE"
                or evidence_fault
                == "MOTOR_RESISTANCE"
            )
        )

        if vision_supports_resistance:
            confidence += (
                reliability
                * self._max_vision_confidence_bonus
            )

        elif str(
            vision_context["status"]
        ) in {
            "DEGRADED",
            "LOW_CONFIDENCE",
        }:
            confidence -= (
                (
                    1.0
                    - reliability
                )
                * self._max_vision_confidence_penalty
            )

        return round(
            max(
                0.0,
                min(
                    confidence,
                    1.0,
                ),
            ),
            4,
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Helpers
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @staticmethod
    def _normalize_fault_type(
        fault_type: object,
    ) -> str:

        raw_fault = getattr(
            fault_type,
            "value",
            fault_type,
        )

        normalized = (
            str(raw_fault)
            .strip()
            .upper()
            .replace(
                "-",
                "_",
            )
        )

        if "." in normalized:
            normalized = normalized.rsplit(
                ".",
                1,
            )[-1]

        if normalized in SUPPORTED_FAULTS:
            return normalized

        return "UNKNOWN"

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_numeric(
        value: object,
        name: str,
    ) -> float:

        try:
            numeric = float(value)
        except (
            TypeError,
            ValueError,
        ) as exc:
            raise TypeError(
                f"{name} must be numeric."
            ) from exc

        if not math.isfinite(
            numeric
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                f"{name} must be finite."
            )

        return numeric

    def _calculate_severity(
        self,
        anomaly_score: float,
    ) -> str:

        if anomaly_score >= self._high_severity_score:
            return "HIGH"

        if anomaly_score >= self._medium_severity_score:
            return "MEDIUM"

        return "LOW"

    @staticmethod
    def _determine_fusion_mode(
        vision_context: dict[str, object],
    ) -> str:

        if not bool(
            vision_context["available"]
        ):
            return "TELEMETRY_ONLY"

        status = str(
            vision_context["status"]
        )

        if status == "HEALTHY":
            return "VISION_AUGMENTED"

        return "VISION_DEGRADED"

    @staticmethod
    def _is_finite_non_negative(
        value: object,
    ) -> bool:

        try:
            numeric_value = float(
                value
            )
        except (
            TypeError,
            ValueError,
        ):
            return False

        return (
            math.isfinite(
                numeric_value
            )
            and numeric_value >= 0.0
        )

    def _build_legacy_main_vision_observation(
        self,
    ) -> None:

        return None


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Human-readable report
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def print_diagnostic_report(
    result: DiagnosticResult,
) -> None:

    print()

    print(
        "ROBOTWIN-AI MULTIMODAL DIAGNOSTIC REPORT"
    )

    print(
        "=" * 72
    )

    print(
        f"Joint             : {result.joint_index}"
    )

    print(
        f"Status            : {result.status}"
    )

    print(
        f"Fault             : {result.fault_type}"
    )

    print(
        f"Severity          : {result.severity}"
    )

    print(
        f"Confidence        : {result.confidence:.4f}"
    )

    print(
        f"Anomaly Score     : {result.anomaly_score:.4f}"
    )

    print()

    print("Telemetry / ML")

    print(
        "-" * 72
    )

    print(
        f"ML Fault          : {result.ml_fault_type}"
    )

    print(
        f"ML Confidence     : {result.ml_confidence:.4f}"
    )

    print()

    print("Vision")

    print(
        "-" * 72
    )

    print(
        f"Vision Available  : {result.vision_available}"
    )

    print(
        f"Vision Status     : {result.vision_status}"
    )

    print(
        f"Vision Reliability: "
        f"{result.vision_reliability:.4f}"
    )

    print(
        f"Fusion Mode       : {result.fusion_mode}"
    )

    print()

    print("Evidence")

    print(
        "-" * 72
    )

    for evidence in result.evidence:
        print(
            f"- {evidence}"
        )

    print()

    print("Vision Evidence")

    print(
        "-" * 72
    )

    if result.vision_evidence:
        for evidence in result.vision_evidence:
            print(
                f"- {evidence}"
            )
    else:
        print(
            "- No vision observation supplied."
        )

    print()

    print("Probable Cause")

    print(
        "-" * 72
    )

    print(
        result.probable_cause
    )

    print()

    print("Recommended Action")

    print(
        "-" * 72
    )

    print(
        result.recommended_action
    )

    print(
        "=" * 72
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Standalone demonstration
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def main() -> None:

    telemetry = JointTelemetry(
        joint_index=2,
        position=0.20,
        velocity=0.05,
        force=80.0,
        motor_current=0.5,
        temperature=90.0,
    )

    brain = DiagnosticBrain()

    result = brain.diagnose(
        telemetry=telemetry,
        target_position=0.80,
    )

    print_diagnostic_report(
        result
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



==================================================
FILE: src\robot_twin\diagnostics\multimodal_diagnostic_brain.py
==================================================
"""
Multimodal diagnostic brain for RoboTwin-AI.

Combines:
    - Telemetry-based diagnosis
    - Vision-derived evidence
    - Vision reliability
    - Reliability-aware multimodal fusion

Design principle:
    Telemetry remains the primary source for internal robot-fault
    diagnosis. Vision provides supporting environmental and motion
    evidence when the visual observation is reliable.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
import math
from typing import Iterable

from robot_twin.diagnostics.diagnostic_brain import (
    DiagnosticBrain,
    DiagnosticResult,
)
from robot_twin.diagnostics.vision_evidence import (
    VisionEvidence,
    VisionEvidenceEngine,
)
from robot_twin.telemetry.robot_adapter import JointTelemetry
from robot_twin.vision.vision_observer import VisionObservation


DEFAULT_VISION_CONFIRMATION_BONUS = 0.10
DEFAULT_VISION_ANOMALY_THRESHOLD = 0.60
DEFAULT_HIGH_RELIABILITY_THRESHOLD = 0.80
DEFAULT_LOW_RELIABILITY_THRESHOLD = 0.50


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MultimodalDiagnosticResult:
    """
    Final diagnostic result after telemetry and vision fusion.
    """

    joint_index: int
    status: str
    fault_type: str
    severity: str
    confidence: float

    telemetry_confidence: float

    vision_score: float
    vision_reliability: float
    combined_score: float

    obstacle_detected: bool

    fusion_mode: str

    evidence: tuple[str, ...]

    probable_cause: str
    recommended_action: str


class MultimodalDiagnosticBrain:
    """
    Reliability-aware multimodal diagnostic engine.

    Telemetry is the primary signal for internal robot faults.

    Vision contributes only when:
        - a valid visual observation exists,
        - the observation has sufficient reliability,
        - the visual evidence is meaningful.

    A healthy visual observation must not reduce telemetry confidence.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        diagnostic_brain: DiagnosticBrain | None = None,
        vision_engine: VisionEvidenceEngine | None = None,
        vision_confirmation_bonus: float = DEFAULT_VISION_CONFIRMATION_BONUS,
        vision_anomaly_threshold: float = DEFAULT_VISION_ANOMALY_THRESHOLD,
        high_reliability_threshold: float = DEFAULT_HIGH_RELIABILITY_THRESHOLD,
        low_reliability_threshold: float = DEFAULT_LOW_RELIABILITY_THRESHOLD,
    ) -> None:
        self.diagnostic_brain = diagnostic_brain or DiagnosticBrain()
        self.vision_engine = vision_engine or VisionEvidenceEngine()

        self.vision_confirmation_bonus = self._validate_ratio(
            vision_confirmation_bonus,
            "vision_confirmation_bonus",
        )

        self.vision_anomaly_threshold = self._validate_ratio(
            vision_anomaly_threshold,
            "vision_anomaly_threshold",
        )

        self.high_reliability_threshold = self._validate_ratio(
            high_reliability_threshold,
            "high_reliability_threshold",
        )

        self.low_reliability_threshold = self._validate_ratio(
            low_reliability_threshold,
            "low_reliability_threshold",
        )

        if self.low_reliability_threshold > self.high_reliability_threshold:
            raise ValueError(
                "low_reliability_threshold cannot be greater than "
                "high_reliability_threshold."
            )

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_ratio(value: float, name: str) -> float:
        """Validate a value expected to be between 0 and 1."""

        if not isinstance(value, (int, float)):
            raise TypeError(f"{name} must be numeric.")

        value = float(value)

        if not math.isfinite(value):
            raise ValueError(f"{name} must be finite.")

        if not 0.0 <= value <= 1.0:
            raise ValueError(f"{name} must be between 0 and 1.")

        return value

    @staticmethod
    def _clamp(
        value: float,
        minimum: float = 0.0,
        maximum: float = 1.0,
    ) -> float:
        """Clamp a numeric value into a safe range."""

        return max(minimum, min(maximum, float(value)))

    def diagnose(
        self,
        diagnostic_result: DiagnosticResult,
        vision_observation: VisionObservation | None = None,
    ) -> MultimodalDiagnosticResult:
        """
        Fuse telemetry diagnosis with optional vision evidence.
        """

        if not isinstance(diagnostic_result, DiagnosticResult):
            raise TypeError(
                "diagnostic_result must be a DiagnosticResult instance."
            )

        vision_evidence = self._build_vision_evidence(
            vision_observation=vision_observation,
        )

        telemetry_confidence = self._safe_confidence(
            diagnostic_result.confidence,
        )

        combined_score = self._calculate_combined_score(
            telemetry_confidence=telemetry_confidence,
            vision_evidence=vision_evidence,
        )

        status = self._select_status(
            telemetry_result=diagnostic_result,
            vision_evidence=vision_evidence,
        )

        fusion_mode = self._determine_fusion_mode(
            telemetry_result=diagnostic_result,
            vision_evidence=vision_evidence,
        )

        evidence = self._build_evidence(
            diagnostic_result=diagnostic_result,
            vision_evidence=vision_evidence,
            fusion_mode=fusion_mode,
        )

        probable_cause = self._build_probable_cause(
            diagnostic_result=diagnostic_result,
            vision_evidence=vision_evidence,
        )

        recommended_action = self._build_recommended_action(
            diagnostic_result=diagnostic_result,
            vision_evidence=vision_evidence,
        )

        return MultimodalDiagnosticResult(
            joint_index=diagnostic_result.joint_index,
            status=status,
            fault_type=diagnostic_result.fault_type,
            severity=diagnostic_result.severity,
            confidence=combined_score,
            telemetry_confidence=telemetry_confidence,
            vision_score=vision_evidence.score,
            vision_reliability=vision_evidence.reliability,
            combined_score=combined_score,
            obstacle_detected=vision_evidence.obstacle_detected,
            fusion_mode=fusion_mode,
            evidence=tuple(evidence),
            probable_cause=probable_cause,
            recommended_action=recommended_action,
        )

    def _build_vision_evidence(
        self,
        vision_observation: VisionObservation | None,
    ) -> VisionEvidence:
        """Convert a vision observation into normalized visual evidence."""

        if vision_observation is None:
            return VisionEvidence(
                obstacle_detected=False,
                abnormal_motion=False,
                visibility_ok=False,
                depth_available=False,
                segmentation_available=False,
                valid_depth_ratio=0.0,
                score=0.0,
                reliability=0.0,
                reasons=(
                    "Vision observation is unavailable.",
                ),
            )

        if not isinstance(vision_observation, VisionObservation):
            raise TypeError(
                "vision_observation must be a VisionObservation instance."
            )

        return self.vision_engine.analyze(
            obstacle_distance=vision_observation.obstacle_distance,
            observed_motion_error=0.0,
            visibility_ok=vision_observation.visibility_ok,
            depth_available=vision_observation.depth_available,
            segmentation_available=(
                vision_observation.segmentation_available
            ),
            valid_depth_ratio=vision_observation.valid_depth_ratio,
        )

    def _calculate_combined_score(
        self,
        telemetry_confidence: float,
        vision_evidence: VisionEvidence,
    ) -> float:
        """
        Calculate reliability-aware confidence.

        A visual score of zero means no visual abnormality.
        It does not mean that the camera is unreliable.
        """

        telemetry_confidence = self._clamp(
            telemetry_confidence,
        )

        if vision_evidence.reliability <= 0.0:
            return telemetry_confidence

        if vision_evidence.score <= 0.0:
            return telemetry_confidence

        visual_bonus = (
            self.vision_confirmation_bonus
            * vision_evidence.score
            * vision_evidence.reliability
        )

        return self._clamp(
            telemetry_confidence + visual_bonus,
        )

    def _select_status(
        self,
        telemetry_result: DiagnosticResult,
        vision_evidence: VisionEvidence,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Select final diagnostic status.

        Telemetry anomaly remains the strongest internal-fault signal.

        A strong and highly reliable visual anomaly can also trigger
        an anomaly state when telemetry itself is normal.
        """

        if telemetry_result.status.upper() == "ANOMALY":
            return "ANOMALY"

        if (
            vision_evidence.score >= self.vision_anomaly_threshold
            and vision_evidence.reliability
            >= self.high_reliability_threshold
        ):
            return "ANOMALY"

        return "NORMAL"

    def _determine_fusion_mode(
        self,
        telemetry_result: DiagnosticResult,
        vision_evidence: VisionEvidence,
    ) -> str:
        """Determine how telemetry and vision contributed."""

        if vision_evidence.reliability <= 0.0:
            return "TELEMETRY_ONLY"

        if vision_evidence.reliability < self.low_reliability_threshold:
            return "VISION_LOW_RELIABILITY"

        telemetry_anomaly = (
            telemetry_result.status.upper() == "ANOMALY"
        )

        strong_visual_evidence = (
            vision_evidence.score >= self.vision_anomaly_threshold
        )

        high_visual_reliability = (
            vision_evidence.reliability
            >= self.high_reliability_threshold
        )

        if (
            telemetry_anomaly
            and strong_visual_evidence
            and high_visual_reliability
        ):
            return "TELEMETRY_CONFIRMED_WITH_VISION"

        if strong_visual_evidence:
            return "VISION_AUGMENTED"

        return "TELEMETRY_PRIMARY"

    @staticmethod
    def _safe_confidence(value: float) -> float:
        """Normalize diagnostic confidence."""

        try:
            value = float(value)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return 0.0

        if not math.isfinite(value):
            return 0.0

        return max(0.0, min(1.0, value))

    def _build_evidence(
        self,
        diagnostic_result: DiagnosticResult,
        vision_evidence: VisionEvidence,
        fusion_mode: str,
    ) -> list[str]:
        """Build human-readable diagnostic evidence."""

        evidence: list[str] = []

        for item in diagnostic_result.evidence:
            evidence.append(str(item))

        evidence.append(
            f"Vision evidence score: {vision_evidence.score:.4f}."
        )

        evidence.append(
            "Vision observation reliability: "
            f"{vision_evidence.reliability:.4f}."
        )

        evidence.append(
            f"Fusion mode: {fusion_mode}."
        )

        for reason in vision_evidence.reasons:
            evidence.append(str(reason))

        return self._unique_strings(evidence)

    def _build_probable_cause(
        self,
        diagnostic_result: DiagnosticResult,
        vision_evidence: VisionEvidence,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Build probable cause.

        Vision provides environmental context and does not independently
        claim an internal mechanical fault.
        """

        base_cause = diagnostic_result.probable_cause

        if (
            vision_evidence.obstacle_detected
            and vision_evidence.reliability
            >= self.low_reliability_threshold
        ):
            obstacle_context = (
                " Camera evidence indicates a nearby obstacle or "
                "environmental interaction that may contribute to "
                "increased mechanical load."
            )

            return f"{base_cause}{obstacle_context}"

        return base_cause

    def _build_recommended_action(
        self,
        diagnostic_result: DiagnosticResult,
        vision_evidence: VisionEvidence,
    ) -> str:
        """Build recommended action with visual safety context."""

        base_action = diagnostic_result.recommended_action

        if (
            vision_evidence.obstacle_detected
            and vision_evidence.reliability
            >= self.low_reliability_threshold
        ):
            obstacle_action = (
                " Verify the robot's workspace for obstacles or "
                "unexpected physical interference before continuing "
                "normal operation."
            )

            return f"{base_action}{obstacle_action}"

        return base_action

    @staticmethod
    def _unique_strings(
        items: Iterable[str],
    ) -> list[str]:
        """Remove duplicate strings while preserving order."""

        unique: list[str] = []
        seen: set[str] = set()

        for item in items:
            normalized = str(item).strip()

            if not normalized:
                continue

            if normalized in seen:
                continue

            seen.add(normalized)
            unique.append(normalized)

        return unique


def main() -> None:
    """
    Run a standalone multimodal diagnostic demonstration.

    Uses a valid JointTelemetry object instead of passing None
    to DiagnosticBrain.
    """

    diagnostic_brain = DiagnosticBrain()
    multimodal_brain = MultimodalDiagnosticBrain()

    telemetry = JointTelemetry(
        joint_index=2,
        position=0.59,
        velocity=0.0,
        force=0.0,
        motor_current=3.325,
        temperature=51.6,
    )

    diagnostic_result = diagnostic_brain.diagnose(
        telemetry=telemetry,
        target_position=telemetry.position,
    )

    vision_observation = VisionObservation(
        obstacle_detected=False,
        obstacle_distance=3.436,
        visibility_ok=True,
        depth_available=True,
        segmentation_available=True,
        valid_depth_ratio=1.0,
        visible_object_count=10,
        image_width=640,
        image_height=480,
        reason=(
            "No obstacle detected inside the configured "
            "safety distance."
        ),
    )

    result = multimodal_brain.diagnose(
        diagnostic_result=diagnostic_result,
        vision_observation=vision_observation,
    )

    print()
    print("ROBOTWIN-AI MULTIMODAL DIAGNOSTIC BRAIN")
    print("=" * 65)

    print(f"Joint                : {result.joint_index}")
    print(f"Status               : {result.status}")
    print(f"Fault                : {result.fault_type}")
    print(f"Severity             : {result.severity}")
    print(
        f"Telemetry confidence : "
        f"{result.telemetry_confidence:.4f}"
    )
    print(
        f"Vision score         : "
        f"{result.vision_score:.4f}"
    )
    print(
        f"Vision reliability   : "
        f"{result.vision_reliability:.4f}"
    )
    print(
        f"Combined confidence : "
        f"{result.combined_score:.4f}"
    )
    print(
        f"Obstacle detected    : "
        f"{result.obstacle_detected}"
    )
    print(
        f"Fusion mode          : "
        f"{result.fusion_mode}"
    )

    print()
    print("Evidence:")

    for item in result.evidence:
        print(f"  - {item}")

    print()
    print("Probable Cause:")
    print(f"  {result.probable_cause}")

    print()
    print("Recommended Action:")
    print(f"  {result.recommended_action}")

    print("=" * 65)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


==================================================
FILE: src\robot_twin\diagnostics\fault_injector.py
==================================================
"""
Fault injection models for RoboTwin-AI.

This module provides controlled actuator-fault simulation for the
RoboTwin-AI digital twin.

The injector preserves the existing RoboTwin-AI telemetry contract so
that PyBulletAdapter, SensorModel, DiagnosticBrain and the temporal
diagnostic pipeline continue to work without modification.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
from typing import Final

import pybullet as p

from robot_twin.simulation.robot_simulator import RobotSimulator


# ======================================================================
# Fault Types
# ======================================================================


class FaultType(str, Enum):
    """Supported simulated robot fault types."""

    NORMAL = "normal"
    MOTOR_RESISTANCE = "motor_resistance"
    LOW_MOTOR_POWER = "low_motor_power"
    POSITION_DRIFT = "position_drift"


# ======================================================================
# Fault Scenario
# ======================================================================


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FaultScenario:
    """Description of one fault injection scenario."""

    fault_type: FaultType
    joint_index: int
    severity: float = 0.0


# ======================================================================
# Fault Telemetry
# ======================================================================


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FaultTelemetry:
    """
    Synthetic fault-side telemetry.

    These fields are intentionally preserved because the existing
    PyBulletAdapter and SensorModel depend on this contract.
    """

    joint_index: int
    fault_type: FaultType
    severity: float

    target_position: float
    effective_target_position: float

    position: float
    velocity: float
    force: float

    fault_load: float
    current_bias: float
    temperature_bias: float


# ======================================================================
# Fault Injector
# ======================================================================


class FaultInjector:
    """
    Apply controlled actuator fault behavior to the simulated robot.

    The injector does not perform diagnosis.

    It creates bounded actuator behavior and synthetic fault signatures.

    Diagnostic interpretation remains the responsibility of:

        SensorModel
            ↓
        BaselineAnomalyDetector
            ↓
        FaultClassifier
            ↓
        FaultEvidenceEngine
            ↓
        DiagnosticBrain
            ↓
        TemporalDiagnosticEngine
    """

    # ==================================================================
    # Nominal target
    # ==================================================================

    DEFAULT_TARGET_POSITION: Final[float] = 0.8

    # ==================================================================
    # Actuator limits
    # ==================================================================

    MAX_MOTOR_FORCE: Final[float] = 40.0

    NORMAL_MOTOR_FORCE: Final[float] = 30.0

    # ==================================================================
    # Bounded velocity controller
    # ==================================================================

    # IMPORTANT:
    #
    # We intentionally use VELOCITY_CONTROL instead of POSITION_CONTROL.
    #
    # PyBullet POSITION_CONTROL can generate large transient velocities
    # for this KUKA configuration. A bounded velocity command gives the
    # digital twin a deterministic actuator command.
    #

    MAX_COMMAND_VELOCITY: Final[float] = 0.75

    POSITION_TO_VELOCITY_GAIN: Final[float] = 1.50

    # ==================================================================
    # Target safety
    # ==================================================================

    MAX_TARGET_MARGIN: Final[float] = 0.02

    # ==================================================================
    # Motor resistance
    # ==================================================================

    MOTOR_RESISTANCE_FORCE_REDUCTION: Final[float] = 0.18

    MOTOR_RESISTANCE_MIN_FORCE: Final[float] = 22.0

    MOTOR_RESISTANCE_TARGET_OFFSET: Final[float] = 0.06

    MOTOR_RESISTANCE_FAULT_LOAD: Final[float] = 4.0

    MOTOR_RESISTANCE_CURRENT_BIAS: Final[float] = 6.2

    MOTOR_RESISTANCE_TEMPERATURE_BIAS: Final[float] = 6.0

    # ==================================================================
    # Low motor power
    # ==================================================================

    LOW_POWER_FORCE_REDUCTION: Final[float] = 0.90

    LOW_POWER_MIN_FORCE: Final[float] = 4.0

    LOW_POWER_TARGET_REDUCTION: Final[float] = 0.45

    LOW_POWER_FAULT_LOAD: Final[float] = 1.0

    # ==================================================================
    # Position drift
    # ==================================================================

    POSITION_DRIFT_TARGET_OFFSET: Final[float] = 0.35

    POSITION_DRIFT_FAULT_LOAD: Final[float] = 0.1

    # ==================================================================
    # Constructor
    # ==================================================================

    def __init__(
        self,
        simulator: RobotSimulator,
    ) -> None:

        self._simulator = simulator

        self._fault_telemetry: dict[
            int,
            FaultTelemetry,
        ] = {}

    # ==================================================================
    # Main API
    # ==================================================================

    def apply(
        self,
        scenario: FaultScenario,
    ) -> FaultTelemetry:
        """
        Apply a fault scenario and return its telemetry contract.

        The return value is required by PyBulletAdapter.apply_fault().
        """

        self._validate_scenario(scenario)

        self._validate_joint(
            scenario.joint_index
        )

        if scenario.fault_type == FaultType.NORMAL:

            telemetry = self._apply_normal(
                scenario
            )

        elif scenario.fault_type == FaultType.MOTOR_RESISTANCE:

            telemetry = self._apply_motor_resistance(
                scenario
            )

        elif scenario.fault_type == FaultType.LOW_MOTOR_POWER:

            telemetry = self._apply_low_motor_power(
                scenario
            )

        elif scenario.fault_type == FaultType.POSITION_DRIFT:

            telemetry = self._apply_position_drift(
                scenario
            )

        else:

            raise ValueError(
                "Unsupported fault type: "
                f"{scenario.fault_type}"
            )

        self._fault_telemetry[
            scenario.joint_index
        ] = telemetry

        return telemetry

    # ==================================================================
    # Backward-compatible API
    # ==================================================================

    def apply_fault(
        self,
        scenario: FaultScenario,
    ) -> FaultTelemetry:
        """Backward-compatible fault injection API."""

        return self.apply(
            scenario
        )

    # ==================================================================
    # NORMAL
    # ==================================================================

    def _apply_normal(
        self,
        scenario: FaultScenario,
    ) -> FaultTelemetry:
        """Apply healthy actuator behavior."""

        target_position = (
            self.DEFAULT_TARGET_POSITION
        )

        effective_target_position = (
            target_position
        )

        self._set_bounded_velocity_control(
            joint_index=scenario.joint_index,
            target_position=(
                effective_target_position
            ),
            force=self.NORMAL_MOTOR_FORCE,
        )

        position, velocity, force = (
            self._read_joint_state(
                scenario.joint_index
            )
        )

        return FaultTelemetry(
            joint_index=scenario.joint_index,
            fault_type=FaultType.NORMAL,
            severity=0.0,
            target_position=target_position,
            effective_target_position=(
                effective_target_position
            ),
            position=position,
            velocity=velocity,
            force=force,
            fault_load=0.0,
            current_bias=0.0,
            temperature_bias=0.0,
        )

    # ==================================================================
    # MOTOR RESISTANCE
    # ==================================================================

    def _apply_motor_resistance(
        self,
        scenario: FaultScenario,
    ) -> FaultTelemetry:
        """Apply simulated motor/mechanical resistance."""

        severity = scenario.severity

        target_position = (
            self.DEFAULT_TARGET_POSITION
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Reduced actuator capability
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        available_force = (
            self.MAX_MOTOR_FORCE
            * (
                1.0
                - (
                    self.MOTOR_RESISTANCE_FORCE_REDUCTION
                    * severity
                )
            )
        )

        available_force = max(
            self.MOTOR_RESISTANCE_MIN_FORCE,
            available_force,
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Small effective target reduction
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        target_offset = (
            self.MOTOR_RESISTANCE_TARGET_OFFSET
            * severity
        )

        effective_target_position = (
            target_position
            - target_offset
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Synthetic mechanical load
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        fault_load = (
            self.MOTOR_RESISTANCE_FAULT_LOAD
            * severity
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Synthetic electrical signature
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        severity_scale = math.sqrt(
            severity
        )

        current_bias = (
            self.MOTOR_RESISTANCE_CURRENT_BIAS
            * severity_scale
        )

        temperature_bias = (
            self.MOTOR_RESISTANCE_TEMPERATURE_BIAS
            * severity_scale
        )

        self._set_bounded_velocity_control(
            joint_index=scenario.joint_index,
            target_position=(
                effective_target_position
            ),
            force=available_force,
        )

        position, velocity, force = (
            self._read_joint_state(
                scenario.joint_index
            )
        )

        return FaultTelemetry(
            joint_index=scenario.joint_index,
            fault_type=FaultType.MOTOR_RESISTANCE,
            severity=severity,
            target_position=target_position,
            effective_target_position=(
                effective_target_position
            ),
            position=position,
            velocity=velocity,
            force=force,
            fault_load=fault_load,
            current_bias=current_bias,
            temperature_bias=temperature_bias,
        )

    # ==================================================================
    # LOW MOTOR POWER
    # ==================================================================

    def _apply_low_motor_power(
        self,
        scenario: FaultScenario,
    ) -> FaultTelemetry:
        """Apply simulated low actuator power."""

        severity = scenario.severity

        target_position = (
            self.DEFAULT_TARGET_POSITION
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Strong actuator-force reduction
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        available_force = (
            self.MAX_MOTOR_FORCE
            * (
                1.0
                - (
                    self.LOW_POWER_FORCE_REDUCTION
                    * severity
                )
            )
        )

        available_force = max(
            self.LOW_POWER_MIN_FORCE,
            available_force,
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Reduced effective target
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        effective_target_position = (
            target_position
            * (
                1.0
                - (
                    self.LOW_POWER_TARGET_REDUCTION
                    * severity
                )
            )
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Synthetic fault load
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        fault_load = (
            self.LOW_POWER_FAULT_LOAD
            * severity
        )

        self._set_bounded_velocity_control(
            joint_index=scenario.joint_index,
            target_position=(
                effective_target_position
            ),
            force=available_force,
        )

        position, velocity, force = (
            self._read_joint_state(
                scenario.joint_index
            )
        )

        return FaultTelemetry(
            joint_index=scenario.joint_index,
            fault_type=FaultType.LOW_MOTOR_POWER,
            severity=severity,
            target_position=target_position,
            effective_target_position=(
                effective_target_position
            ),
            position=position,
            velocity=velocity,
            force=force,
            fault_load=fault_load,
            current_bias=0.0,
            temperature_bias=0.0,
        )

    # ==================================================================
    # POSITION DRIFT
    # ==================================================================

    def _apply_position_drift(
        self,
        scenario: FaultScenario,
    ) -> FaultTelemetry:
        """Apply simulated position-feedback drift."""

        severity = scenario.severity

        target_position = (
            self.DEFAULT_TARGET_POSITION
        )

        effective_target_position = (
            target_position
            + (
                self.POSITION_DRIFT_TARGET_OFFSET
                * severity
            )
        )

        fault_load = (
            self.POSITION_DRIFT_FAULT_LOAD
            * severity
        )

        self._set_bounded_velocity_control(
            joint_index=scenario.joint_index,
            target_position=(
                effective_target_position
            ),
            force=self.MAX_MOTOR_FORCE,
        )

        position, velocity, force = (
            self._read_joint_state(
                scenario.joint_index
            )
        )

        return FaultTelemetry(
            joint_index=scenario.joint_index,
            fault_type=FaultType.POSITION_DRIFT,
            severity=severity,
            target_position=target_position,
            effective_target_position=(
                effective_target_position
            ),
            position=position,
            velocity=velocity,
            force=force,
            fault_load=fault_load,
            current_bias=0.0,
            temperature_bias=0.0,
        )

    # ==================================================================
    # BOUNDED VELOCITY CONTROLLER
    # ==================================================================

    def _set_bounded_velocity_control(
        self,
        joint_index: int,
        target_position: float,
        force: float,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Apply a deterministic bounded velocity command.

        Instead of asking PyBullet to directly solve a position target,
        we convert position error into a velocity command.

        position error
              ↓
        proportional gain
              ↓
        velocity command
              ↓
        clamp to ±MAX_COMMAND_VELOCITY
              ↓
        PyBullet VELOCITY_CONTROL
        """

        lower, upper = (
            self._get_joint_limits(
                joint_index
            )
        )

        safe_lower = (
            lower
            + self.MAX_TARGET_MARGIN
        )

        safe_upper = (
            upper
            - self.MAX_TARGET_MARGIN
        )

        safe_target = self._clamp(
            target_position,
            safe_lower,
            safe_upper,
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Read current position
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        state = p.getJointState(
            self._simulator.robot_id,
            joint_index,
            physicsClientId=(
                self._simulator.client_id
            ),
        )

        current_position = float(
            state[0]
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Position error
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        position_error = (
            safe_target
            - current_position
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Convert position error into velocity command
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        target_velocity = (
            position_error
            * self.POSITION_TO_VELOCITY_GAIN
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # HARD command limit
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        target_velocity = self._clamp(
            target_velocity,
            -self.MAX_COMMAND_VELOCITY,
            self.MAX_COMMAND_VELOCITY,
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Force limit
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        safe_force = self._clamp(
            force,
            0.0,
            self.MAX_MOTOR_FORCE,
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Velocity control
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        p.setJointMotorControl2(
            bodyUniqueId=(
                self._simulator.robot_id
            ),
            jointIndex=joint_index,
            controlMode=p.VELOCITY_CONTROL,
            targetVelocity=target_velocity,
            force=safe_force,
            physicsClientId=(
                self._simulator.client_id
            ),
        )

    # ==================================================================
    # Joint state
    # ==================================================================

    def _read_joint_state(
        self,
        joint_index: int,
    ) -> tuple[
        float,
        float,
        float,
    ]:
        """Read current PyBullet joint state."""

        state = p.getJointState(
            self._simulator.robot_id,
            joint_index,
            physicsClientId=(
                self._simulator.client_id
            ),
        )

        position = float(
            state[0]
        )

        velocity = float(
            state[1]
        )

        # Existing RoboTwin-AI contract:
        # state[3] = applied motor torque.
        #
        # Keep the field name "force" because the rest of the
        # RoboTwin-AI architecture already depends on it.
        force = float(
            state[3]
        )

        return (
            position,
            velocity,
            force,
        )

    # ==================================================================
    # Joint limits
    # ==================================================================

    def _get_joint_limits(
        self,
        joint_index: int,
    ) -> tuple[float, float]:
        """Read actual URDF joint limits."""

        if hasattr(
            self._simulator,
            "get_joint_limits",
        ):

            limits = (
                self._simulator.get_joint_limits(
                    joint_index
                )
            )

            lower = float(
                limits[0]
            )

            upper = float(
                limits[1]
            )

            if (
                math.isfinite(lower)
                and math.isfinite(upper)
                and lower < upper
            ):
                return (
                    lower,
                    upper,
                )

        return (
            -2.5,
            2.5,
        )

    # ==================================================================
    # Public telemetry
    # ==================================================================

    def get_fault_telemetry(
        self,
        joint_index: int,
    ) -> FaultTelemetry:
        """Return the last generated fault telemetry."""

        if joint_index in self._fault_telemetry:
            return self._fault_telemetry[
                joint_index
            ]

        return FaultTelemetry(
            joint_index=joint_index,
            fault_type=FaultType.NORMAL,
            severity=0.0,
            target_position=(
                self.DEFAULT_TARGET_POSITION
            ),
            effective_target_position=(
                self.DEFAULT_TARGET_POSITION
            ),
            position=0.0,
            velocity=0.0,
            force=0.0,
            fault_load=0.0,
            current_bias=0.0,
            temperature_bias=0.0,
        )

    def get_target_position(
        self,
        joint_index: int,
    ) -> float:
        """Return the diagnostic target position."""

        telemetry = self.get_fault_telemetry(
            joint_index
        )

        return telemetry.target_position

    # ==================================================================
    # Cleanup
    # ==================================================================

    def clear(
        self,
        joint_index: int,
    ) -> None:
        """Clear one joint's fault state."""

        self._validate_joint_index(
            joint_index
        )

        self._fault_telemetry.pop(
            joint_index,
            None,
        )

        p.setJointMotorControl2(
            bodyUniqueId=(
                self._simulator.robot_id
            ),
            jointIndex=joint_index,
            controlMode=p.VELOCITY_CONTROL,
            targetVelocity=0.0,
            force=0.0,
            physicsClientId=(
                self._simulator.client_id
            ),
        )

    def clear_all(self) -> None:
        """Clear all fault states."""

        joint_indices = list(
            self._fault_telemetry.keys()
        )

        for joint_index in joint_indices:
            self.clear(
                joint_index
            )

        self._fault_telemetry.clear()

    # ==================================================================
    # Validation
    # ==================================================================

    def _validate_joint(
        self,
        joint_index: int,
    ) -> None:
        """Validate joint index and joint type."""

        self._validate_joint_index(
            joint_index
        )

        joint_count = p.getNumJoints(
            self._simulator.robot_id,
            self._simulator.client_id,
        )

        if joint_index >= joint_count:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Joint index {joint_index} "
                f"does not exist. "
                f"Robot contains "
                f"{joint_count} joints."
            )

        joint_info = p.getJointInfo(
            self._simulator.robot_id,
            joint_index,
            physicsClientId=(
                self._simulator.client_id
            ),
        )

        if (
            joint_info[2]
            != p.JOINT_REVOLUTE
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                f"Joint {joint_index} "
                "is not a revolute joint."
            )

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_joint_index(
        joint_index: int,
    ) -> None:
        """Validate joint index."""

        if not isinstance(
            joint_index,
            int,
        ):
            raise TypeError(
